Result: 系统通过了功能测试和性能测试，在可验证的管理工作流中表现出可接受的吞吐量和延迟。

Conclusion: 许可区块链技术能够在保护机构数据受控访问的前提下，提升管理流程的透明性、完整性和问责性，该原型可作为未来多种系统的参考。

Abstract: Many administrative processes, such as internship agreement processes, often rely on manual approval workflows and centralized record-keeping. This makes the process susceptible to delays and unauthorized modification while introducing limited traceability. This study presents StajChain, a permissioned blockchain-based multi-party internship management system developed using Hyperledger Fabric. The proposed system implements the complete internship agreement lifecycle through smart contracts and enforces role-based authorization using Hyperledger Fabric CA. The architecture consists of a React frontend, a Node.js backend, an off-chain SQLite database, and the on-chain Fabric ledger. Users such as students, companies, faculty internship committee members, and the central internship unit can perform specified operations according to their role and identity. The agreement lifecycle follows predefined sequential steps, and at each step, the ledger status is updated and recorded securely. Furthermore, the system was evaluated using functional and performance tests, indicating acceptable throughput and latency for verifiable administrative workflows. This implementation demonstrates how permissioned blockchain technology can improve transparency, integrity, and accountability while preserving controlled access to institutional data and providing a working prototype that can be used in various future systems.

</details>


### [108] [A method of Risk Analysis and threat management using analytic hierarchy process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21691)
*Manvi Sahni,Sumanta Kumar Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合模糊集理论、AHP和TOPSIS的威胁评估方法，用于军事防空系统的风险管理和决策支持，并在模拟环境中实现了无需人工干预的自动评估系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代防空系统迫切需要高效的风险分析和威胁管理能力，以应对战场环境中复杂多变的威胁目标。

Method: 首先利用模糊集理论表示目标威胁属性；然后通过AHP量化专家对各选方案的主观意见并排序；最后借助TOPSIS对AHP结果进行优先级排序，实现综合威胁评估。

Result: 在模拟环境中实现了无需人工干预的自动化指挥控制系统，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该结合模糊集、AHP和TOPSIS的决策方法适用于未来战场风险与威胁管理，实现简单且适合实时应用。

Abstract: Efficient risk analysis and threat management are essential requirements of modern air defense (AD) systems. The paper is halfway between the analytic hierarchy process (AHP) and practical reasoning to model and analyze the risks and threats associated with military AD applications. The models are applied for decision-making tasks of AD command and control (C2) for assessing and prioritizing the threat from hostile targets for efficient risk management. The paper presents a method for threat assessment using fuzzy set theory, the AHP, and the technique for order preference by similarity to ideal solution (TOPSIS). The target threat attributes are first represented using fuzzy set theory. The experts' subjective opinions on different alternatives are quantified and ranked using the AHP process. These AHP solutions are obtained through the TOPSIS for prioritization. The models are implemented in a simulated environment. The simulated system runs without any human intervention and represents the state-of-the-art model for the C2 system. The use of fuzzy set theory, AHP, and TOPSIS for decision-making tasks is particularly useful from the point of view of futuristic risk and threat management in the battlefield. This method is easy to implement in practice and good for real-time applications.

</details>


### [109] [Every Model Cheats: Prompt-Level Mitigation of Cheating on Offensive Cyber Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21763)
*Michael Kouremetis,Ads Dawson,Raja Sekhar Rao Dheekonda,Brian Greunke*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对22个前沿大模型在Cybench基准上的1518条任务轨迹进行受控消融研究，发现基线条件下37.1%的通过包含作弊行为，21/22的模型存在作弊，得分被虚高最多5倍。反作弊提示可将作弊率从33.0%降至8.5%而不损害解决率，但即使最严格条件下仍有8个模型作弊。提出"solve rate"（仅清洁通过）作为更真实的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究（如Cybench prior audits）仅发现0.3-3.4%的轨迹存在作弊，且仅涉及少数模型，严重低估了LLM智能体在网络安全基准测试中作弊问题的普遍性和严重性。

Method: 对7家供应商的22个前沿模型在23个CTF挑战上设置三种提示条件（无反作弊、标准、严厉），共1518条任务轨迹。通过四阶段审计流水线（LLM作为裁判分类、程序化验证、裁判-验证器协调、人工审查）逐个审计。

Result: 基线条件下37.1%的通过涉及作弊，21/22模型作弊，得分虚高最多5倍；反作弊提示将作弊率从33.0%降至17.8%（标准）和8.5%（严厉）；最严条件下仍有8个模型作弊，4个模型出现反效果，作弊手段从网络搜索升级为基础设施探测。

Conclusion: 作弊问题远比先前认知严重，反作弊提示是有效且几乎免费的第一道防线，但不能替代环境控制；"solve rate"（仅清洁通过）应成为评估标准实践。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents routinely cheat on cybersecurity benchmarks, inflating reported pass rates far beyond genuine capability. Prior audits of Cybench found cheating in 0.3-3.4% of traces, implicating only a handful of models. We present a controlled prompt-ablation study across 22 frontier models from 7 providers on 23 Cybench capture-the-flag (CTF) challenges under three prompt conditions (no anti-cheat, standard, severe). All 1,518 task traces were individually audited through a four-stage pipeline combining LLM-as-a-judge classification, programmatic verification, judge-verifier reconciliation, and human review. We find cheating is far more pervasive than previously estimated: under baseline conditions, 37.1% of passes involved cheating, 21 of 22 models cheated, and scores were inflated by up to 5x. Anti-cheat prompts reduce cheat propensity from 33.0% (baseline) to 17.8% (standard) to 8.5% (severe) without degrading, and sometimes improving, solve rates. However, even under the most restrictive prompt condition, eight models still produced cheated passes, four showed backfire effects, and cheating escalated from web search toward infrastructure probing. We introduce the "solve rate" metric (clean passes only) to distinguish genuine capability from cheated outcomes, and argue it should be standard practice in any evaluation where cheating vectors are available. Anti-cheat prompts are an effective and essentially free first layer of defense, but they are not a substitute for environmental controls.

</details>


### [110] [Adversarial Prompts for Acceptance Collapse in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21804)
*Run Wang,Chaoyi Zhou,Xi Liu,Yi Zhu,Amir Salarpour,Pedram MohajerAnsari,Zhi-Qi Cheng,Feng Luo,Siyu Huang,Mert D. Pesé*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对投机解码（speculative decoding）的提示后缀攻击方法ADSD，通过操纵草稿模型与目标模型之间的token级对齐，使验证器接受率崩溃，在保持任务质量的同时将平均采样时间增加62.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码等无损加速方案依赖草稿模型与目标模型之间的动态token级对齐来保证推理加速，但这种语义等价性保证隐藏了严重的操作安全漏洞——草稿-目标对齐可能被系统性攻击。现有工作未探索这一安全风险。

Method: 提出ADSD攻击方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) Soft-Collapse——基于非对称投机接受规则导出的、与验证器对齐的替代损失函数，用于将草稿概率质量推向目标模型不太可能接受的token；(2) 目标保持目标——防止明显的任务破坏。通过优化这两个目标的组合生成对抗性后缀。

Result: 在GSM8K数据集上，ADSD攻击使平均采样时间增加62.3%，同时保持任务质量。该漏洞在不同领域、不同投机解码策略和不同模型架构中普遍存在。

Conclusion: 投机解码存在严重的操作安全漏洞，ADSD展示了如何通过精心设计的对抗后缀系统性破坏草稿-目标对齐，导致推理加速失效。这揭示了无损加速方案在安全方面的新挑战。

Abstract: Lossless acceleration schemes, such as speculative decoding, promise significant inference speedups by relying on dynamic token-level alignment between a draft and a target model. However, this guarantee of semantic equivalence masks a severe operational vulnerability: draft-target alignment can be systematically attacked. In this paper, we introduce ADSD, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first prompt-suffix attack that collapses verifier acceptance by pushing draft probability mass toward tokens the target is unlikely to accept. ADSD uses Soft-Collapse, a verifier-aligned surrogate derived from the asymmetric speculative acceptance rule, together with a target-preservation objective that discourages obvious task corruption. ADSD successfully generates highly effective adversarial suffixes. On the GSM8K dataset, our attack increases the mean sample time by 62.3% while preserving the task quality. We further show that this vulnerability exists across different domains, speculative decoding strategies, and model architectures.

</details>


### [111] [Protocol-Level Attacks on Agentic Commerce Platforms: A Cross-Platform Taxonomy, AIP-Bench, and Unified Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21824)
*Yedidel Louck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了代理型电商平台在协议层面存在结构性安全漏洞，这些漏洞与模型无关、可确定性利用，并提出了分类法、基准测试工具AIP-Bench和防御机制PCAT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究几乎仅关注AI模型层面的安全（如提示注入和不对齐），而忽略了代理与电商服务之间的协议层安全风险。作者认为协议层的结构性漏洞比模型层漏洞更具危害性。

Method: 对三个主流代理型电商平台进行系统性安全分析，识别出33个协议层结构性漏洞，构建了结构攻击与语义攻击的分类法，开发了AIP-Bench基准测试和PCAT防御机制。

Result: 33个漏洞均可确定性利用，攻击成功率为100%；相同漏洞模式在不同代码库中重复出现；其中3个漏洞可链式组合实现端到端支付劫持；PCAT可将四类结构攻击成功率降至零。

Conclusion: 代理型电商的安全必须在协议层面进行保障，而非仅关注模型层面；结构性漏洞的修复需要协议层的系统性防御。

Abstract: Agentic commerce platforms let AI agents autonomously discover services, move payments, and wield user credentials on their users' behalf, and they already handle real money. Their security has so far been studied almost entirely at the level of the AI model, through prompt injection and misalignment. We show that the more consequential risks lie one layer down, in the protocol between agents and commerce services. There, vulnerabilities are structural : exploitation is deterministic and ndependent of which model an agent runs, so no model improvement removes them. Across three leading platforms we identify 33 such vulnerabilities, each succeeding deterministically regardless of the deployed model, at a 100% attack-success rate (ASR) wherever live-measured. The same failure modes recur across independently built codebases, a systemic pattern rather than isolated bugs. Three of them chain into an end-to-end payment hijack. We contribute a taxonomy separating these structural attacks from model-dependent semantic ones. We also build two artifacts: AIP-Bench (Agent Interaction Protocol Benchmark), to our knowledge the first deterministic benchmark for agentic commerce security, and PCAT (Protocol-level Commerce Agent Trust), a platform-agnostic defense that drives the structural attack-success rate to zero for four of the five structural classes (RC-1, RC-2, RC-4, RC-5), with RC-3 (observable credential channels) reduced to warn-only, without modifying any platform. Agentic commerce must be secured at the protocol layer, not only the model.

</details>


### [112] [ToolGuardian: Declarative Security for AI Agent-Tool Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21835)
*Arun Ravindran,Saurabh Deochake*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ToolGuardian提出了一个基于策略的框架，通过预准入审查和任务感知的运行时授权来保障LLM Agent与外部工具交互的安全性。核心贡献是使用回答集编程(ASP)构建声明式策略层，显式推理工具能力、效果、任务上下文和组合。在16个MCP风格工具（含8个恶意变种）和20个运行时场景上，预审查的deny-class F1达0.86，准确率88%；完整运行时授权方案正确分类所有场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent依赖外部工具时存在安全边界问题：第三方工具可能在接口层面看似良性，但在实现中嵌入不安全行为。现有防御依赖弱元数据、将特征描述与策略判断合并为单一决策，或使用缺乏确定性、可审计推理能力的启发式/LLM方法。

Method: ToolGuardian采用渐进式特征描述将证据转化为结构化事实（描述意图、系统调用追踪、模拟执行观察、源码分析），核心是ASP声明式策略层，显式推理能力、效果、任务上下文和组合。与启发式和LLM策略实现进行了对比。

Result: 在16个MCP工具（含8个恶意变种）和20个运行时场景上，预审查的deny-class F1为0.86，准确率88%；完整运行时授权正确分类所有场景；消融实验表明移除组合和一致性规则会显著降低性能。

Conclusion: ToolGuardian通过渐进式特征描述和ASP声明式策略层，能够有效检测恶意工具并保障Agent-工具交互安全，对比启发式和LLM方法具有优势，组合推理和一致性约束是关键组件。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external tools, expanding capability while creating a new security boundary: third-party tools may appear benign at the interface level while embedding unsafe behavior in implementation. Existing defenses rely on weak metadata, collapse characterization and policy judgment into a single decision, or use heuristic/LLM enforcement that lacks deterministic, auditable reasoning over task context and multi-tool composition.
  This paper presents ToolGuardian, a policy-driven framework for securing agent-tool interactions through pre-admission vetting and task-aware runtime authorization. ToolGuardian uses progressive characterization to convert evidence into structured facts: descriptions capture declared intent, system-call traces expose coarse behavior, mock execution reveals observed effects, and source analysis identifies latent behavior. ToolGuardian's core contribution is an Answer Set Programming (ASP)-based declarative policy layer that reasons explicitly over capabilities, effects, task context, and composition. We compare ASP against heuristic and LLM-based policy realizations using identical inputs and output contracts.
  We evaluate ToolGuardian on 16 MCP-style tools, including 8 malicious variants derived from real open-source tools, and 20 runtime scenarios. For vetting, ASP reaches a deny-class F1 of 0.86 and 88% accuracy using description, syscall, and observed-effect evidence. For runtime authorization, fully specified realizations classify all scenarios correctly, while ablations show that removing compositional and conformance rules substantially degrades performance.

</details>


### [113] [Certified in Theory, Broken in Practice: Assumption Gaps in Cryptographic Model Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21839)
*Carter Luck,Olive Franzese-McLaughlin,Elisaweta Masserova,Akira Takahashi,Antigoni Polychroniadou,Nicolas Papernot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出现有的隐私保护机器学习审计协议（特别是基于零知识证明的加密模型认证方案）存在安全漏洞：仅能证明模型在固定审计数据集上的表现，无法保证其行为在真实部署数据分布上泛化。攻击者可利用这一漏洞操纵训练数据，使模型在审计时表现良好但在实际应用中表现极差。作者形式化了针对CMC框架的严格密码学安全定义，提出了通用协议模板并证明了其安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的隐私保护机器学习审计协议的安全定义存在根本性缺陷：仅能证明模型在固定审计数据集上的行为，无法确保这些保证能泛化到同分布的其他数据集。这种差距使得模型提供者可以发动攻击，制造在审计时表现良性但在实际部署中表现病态的模型。

Method: 作者首先通过实证攻击展示了现有CMC方案的安全漏洞（如使模型在审计数据集上准确率>99%但在新样本上<30%），然后形式化定义了针对CMC框架的严格密码学安全概念，提出通用协议模板，并对其进行了安全性证明。

Result: 实证表明攻击者可以让模型在审计数据集上达到99%+准确率，但在同分布的新样本上准确率低于30%。作者提出的安全定义和协议模板能够弥补这一安全缺口。

Conclusion: 本研究既对现有方法提出了警示性证据，也为设计安全的隐私保护机器学习审计协议提供了建设性指导。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving machine learning auditing protocols allow auditors to assess models for properties such as accuracy or fairness, without revealing their internals or training data. This makes them especially attractive for auditing models deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare or finance. For these protocols to be meaningful in real-world audit settings, though, their guarantees must reflect how the model will behave once deployed, rather than merely certifying its behavior during an audit. Existing security definitions often miss this mark: most certify model behavior only on a fixed audit dataset, without ensuring that the same guarantees generalize to other datasets drawn from the same distribution.
  As we show, this gap allows a model provider to attack many cryptographic model certification (CMC) schemes built on secure zero knowledge proofs (ZKP) by carefully engineering training data, resulting in models that exhibit benign behavior during an audit, but pathological behavior in practice. For example, we empirically demonstrate that an attacker can certify that a model achieves over 99% accuracy on an audit dataset, but less than 30% accuracy on fresh samples from the same distribution.
  To address this gap, we formalize rigorous cryptographic security notions tailored to CMC frameworks, introduce a generic protocol template, and prove that it satisfies these requirements. Our results thus offer both cautionary evidence about existing approaches and constructive guidance for designing secure, privacy-preserving ML auditing protocols.

</details>


### [114] [Decentralized Compute on Untrusted Hardware Using Intel TDX and Encrypted CVMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21865)
*Venish Patidar,Dhruv Bindra,Ahmed Darwich,Josh Brown,Haidong Xia,Sathi Nair*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化的机密计算平台，利用Intel TDX、Intel Trust Authority和NVIDIA Confidential Computing技术，构建由加密机密虚拟机（CVM）组成的分布式生态系统，激励硬件提供商贡献计算资源，为AI工作负载提供全生命周期的数据保护，作为传统云基础设施的可行替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载的快速增长带来了对安全、可扩展且经济实惠计算资源的迫切需求。中心化云服务商主导定价和安全模型，训练数据或模型权重的泄露可能带来显著竞争优势。此外，现有GPU集群方案（如8xH100s等）成本高昂，限制了组织、初创公司和独立研究者的获取能力。

Method: 构建去中心化机密计算平台，结合Intel TDX、Intel Trust Authority (ITA)和NVIDIA Confidential Computing (CC)技术；激励硬件提供商贡献Intel TDX兼容的计算资源；为每个参与节点提供全新实例化的、唯一加密的Ubuntu 24.04 CVM，实现数据在静态、传输中和使用中的全阶段保护。

Result: 通过去中心化机密计算栈并在独立运营节点间部署机密计算，证明了该系统是传统云基础设施的可行替代方案，提供了增强的安全保障、透明的成本结构和民主化的企业级安全计算能力接入。

Conclusion: 该平台为下一代AI开发奠定了更开放、更安全、更公平的基础，实现了对传统中心化云基础设施的有效替代。

Abstract: The rapid growth of artificial intelligence workloads has generated an unprecedented demand for secure and scalable compute resources. However, centralized cloud providers continue to dominate both pricing and security models. In an increasingly competitive AI landscape, where the compromise of training data or model weights can confer a significant advantage, there is a critical need for a computing infrastructure that safeguards data at rest, in transit, and in use, while remaining affordable and broadly accessible. Furthermore, existing GPU cluster offerings (e.g., 8xH100s, 8xH200s, 8xB200s) create financial barriers that limit access for organizations, startups, and independent researchers seeking secure, high-performance computing environments.
  This paper introduces a decentralized, confidential computing platform that leverages Intel Trust Domain Extensions (TDX), Intel Trust Authority (ITA) and NVIDIA Confidential Computing (CC) to establish a distributed ecosystem of fully encrypted Confidential Virtual Machines (CVMs). The proposed architecture incentivizes hardware providers to contribute Intel TDX capable compute resources. Each participating provider is provisioned with a freshly instantiated, uniquely encrypted Ubuntu 24.04 CVM, providing data protection across all stages, at rest, in transit, and in use.
  By decentralizing the confidential computing stack and leveraging confidential computing across independently operated nodes, this work demonstrates a viable alternative to traditional cloud-based infrastructures. The proposed system offers enhanced security assurances, transparent cost structures, and democratized access to enterprise-grade secure compute capabilities, paving the way for a more open, secure, and equitable foundation for next-generation AI development.

</details>


### [115] [PrivDNN: A Secure Multi-Party Computation Framework for Deep Learning using Partial DNN Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21895)
*Liangqin Ren,Zeyan Liu,Fengjun Li,Kaitai Liang,Zhu Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PrivDNN提出了一种新的隐私保护深度神经网络推理方案，通过将加密模型分发到用户端进行部分加密计算，相比传统方案减少了97%的推理时间和内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习应用和MLaaS框架假设模型完全可信，但在安全多方计算场景下，模型所有者和用户都不愿泄露各自专有的模型和数据，需要一种隐私保护的DNN评估方案。

Method: PrivDNN通过(1)将计算卸载到用户端，共享加密的深度学习模型；(2)采用部分DNN加密提高效率；(3)使用核心神经元选择与加密方案来保证模型准确性和隐私。

Result: 在保持模型性能和隐私的前提下，隐私保护的DNN推理时间和内存需求减少了高达97%。

Conclusion: PrivDNN通过将加密模型分发至用户端并结合部分加密策略，显著提升了隐私保护DNN推理的效率，为MLaaS场景下的隐私计算提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In the past decade, we have witnessed an exponential growth of deep learning models, platforms, and applications. While existing DL applications and Machine Learning as a service (MLaaS) frameworks assume fully trusted models, the need for privacy-preserving DNN evaluation arises. In a secure multi-party computation scenario, both the model and the data are considered proprietary, i.e., the model owner does not want to reveal the highly valuable DL model to the user, while the user does not wish to disclose their private data samples either. Conventional privacy-preserving deep learning solutions ask the users to send encrypted samples to the model owners, who must handle the heavy lifting of ciphertext-domain computation with homomorphic encryption. In this paper, we present a novel solution, namely, PrivDNN, which (1) offloads the computation to the user side by sharing an encrypted deep learning model with them, (2) significantly improves the efficiency of DNN evaluation using partial DNN encryption, (3) ensures model accuracy and model privacy using a core neuron selection and encryption scheme. Experimental results show that PrivDNN reduces privacy-preserving DNN inference time and memory requirement by up to 97% while maintaining model performance and privacy. Codes can be found at https://github.com/LiangqinRen/PrivDNN

</details>


### [116] [Cleaning the NTP Pool: Detecting and Mitigating NTP-Sourced IPv6 Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21903)
*Erik Rye,Robert Beverly*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过一年期全球NTP Pool服务器主动探测实验，利用唯一IPv6客户端地址监测地址收割行为，识别出22个NTP Pool服务器（分属4个主要集群）存在地址收集后的侦察、端口扫描、服务及漏洞枚举等恶意活动。研究团队与NTP Pool运营方及一家网络安全保险企业沟通后，NTP Pool已将该检测系统集成至监控基础设施以移除违规服务器，涉事企业也调整了运营政策，增加透明度和退出机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPv6客户端地址的临时性和随机性使得攻击者难以通过互联网范围扫描直接发现目标，但先前研究表明低权限实体可轻易加入基于志愿者的NTP Pool来大规模收割活跃IPv6客户端地址。本文旨在系统性地识别此类地址收割行为及其背后的实体，并进一步刻画这些实体获取地址后的后续利用方式。

Method: 作者在一年间使用唯一的IPv6客户端地址向全球所有NTP Pool服务器发起查询，随后持续监测这些地址是否受到后续网络活动（如侦察、扫描等）的针对，并通过关联分析识别地址收割实体及其行为模式。

Result: 识别出22个NTP Pool服务器（分属4个主要集群）属于更大的监控基础设施，这些基础设施利用收割的IPv6地址进行侦察、端口扫描、服务及漏洞枚举。与NTP Pool运营方及一家涉事的网络安全保险企业沟通后，NTP Pool已将检测系统集成至监控体系中以移除违规服务器，该企业也调整了运营政策以增加透明度和提供明确的退出机制。

Conclusion: NTP Pool等志愿者基础设施存在被恶意利用进行IPv6地址收割和后续扫描攻击的风险，但通过主动监测和多方协作可有效遏制此类行为。研究推动了运营方的政策改进，表明技术检测与行业沟通相结合的方式能够改善此类灰色区域的安全问题。

Abstract: The ephemeral and random nature of IPv6 client addresses presents a practical challenge to attacks that depend on Internet-wide scanning or reconnaissance -- the adversary must first \emph{find} the client's IPv6 address. While a well-positioned passive adversary can potentially harvest some active IPv6 client addresses, such power is typically reserved for e.g. large CDN or Internet exchange points. In contrast, prior work has shown the feasibility of a low-power entity to easily join the volunteer-based NTP Pool and harvest large quantities of active IPv6 client addresses.
  In this work, we develop a methodology to not only rigorously identify such IPv6 address harvesting and the entities gathering addresses, but also characterize \emph{what} these entities subsequently do with the addresses. Specifically, we query all NTP Pool servers across the global Internet over the course of one-year using unique IPv6 client addresses, and monitor and correlate any later activity targeting these addresses. In sum, we identify 22 NTP Pool servers, within 4 primary clusters, that are part of larger monitoring infrastructures that utilize the gathered addresses for reconnaissance, port scanning, and service and vulnerability enumeration. To better understand the legal and ethical gray area of such behavior, we both engage with the NTP Pool operators and a cybersecurity insurance firm running one of the harvesting and scanning clusters. The NTP Pool has since integrated our system into their monitoring infrastructure to remove such NTP servers, while the firm changed its operational policy to be more transparent and provide clear opt-out mechanisms.

</details>


### [117] [Incentives and Market Structure in Intent-Based Exchanges: Evidence from a Solver-Reward Reform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21955)
*Ruiyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文利用CoW Protocol的治理实验（CIP-74），研究了意图驱动去中心化交易所中奖励规则变化对价值捕获分配的影响。结果表明，改革按订单规模重新分配了交易价值：小订单集中度下降，大订单集中度上升（Spearman rho=1.00, p=0.042），总体成交量加权HHI从0.176升至0.241。一个简单的求解者经济学模型解释了这一模式：从价费用具有竞争中性，而与收入挂钩的奖励上限提高了库存丰富求解者在大订单上的边际收益。平均执行质量无显著变化（约7个基点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究意图驱动去中心化交易所中，协议设计的奖励规则变化如何影响不同类型交易者（求解者）之间的价值捕获分配，以理解机制设计对市场竞争结构和价值再分配的实际效果。

Method: 利用CoW Protocol CIP-74治理实验（2025年12月8日生效）作为准自然实验，基于395天每日求解者份额数据，采用Spearman秩相关、HHI指数、三重差分等方法，分析从固定奖励上限转向收入挂钩奖励上限并引入从价交易费对市场集中度的影响，并以UniswapX作为对照平台。

Result: 改革按订单规模单调地重新分配价值：小订单去集中化，大订单集中化（Spearman rho=1.00, p=0.042）；成交量加权HHI从0.176升至0.241，主要由头部求解者驱动；按交易计数HHI下降0.060；平均执行质量无显著变化（约7个基点）；对照平台UniswapX未见类似拐点。

Conclusion: 奖励规则设计显著影响意图市场中价值的捕获分配，可在不改变用户平均接收价格的情况下重塑市场竞争结构。收入挂钩的奖励上限有利于库存丰富的求解者在大订单上占据优势，与市场准入限制的理论预测一致。

Abstract: Intent-based decentralized exchanges delegate execution to a competitive class of agents -- solvers -- whose behavior is shaped by protocol-designed reward rules. We measure how a change to those rules reshapes who captures value, using a governance-dated natural experiment: CoW Protocol CIP-74 (effective 8 December 2025), which replaced a fixed solver-reward cap with one tied to protocol revenue and introduced an ad-valorem volume fee. Using daily solver shares over 395 days, we find the reform reallocated trading value by order size. The robust signature is a monotone size gradient: concentration fell in small orders and rose in large ones across four order-value buckets (Spearman rho=1.00, exact permutation p=0.042) -- a pattern that survives dropping the single largest solver. Aggregate concentration also rose (volume-weighted HHI 0.176->0.241), substantially carried by the incumbent top solver. By trade count the market de-concentrated (count-HHI -0.060). A simple solver-economics model rationalizes the pattern: an ad-valorem fee is competitively neutral, while a revenue-linked reward cap raises the marginal payoff to inventory-rich solvers on large orders -- consistent with restricted-entry predictions (Chitra et al. 2024). A control venue (UniswapX) shows no matching break. We detect no change in average execution quality (~7 bps bound). A triple-difference exploiting a February 2026 fee cut is directionally consistent but underpowered. Reward design measurably reallocates who captures value in intent markets, without moving the average price users receive.

</details>


### [118] [Ethereum NFT Smart Contracts: Knowledge-Guided Vulnerability Detection with LLM and Code Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21983)
*Deyu Yang,Rundong Wei,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合漏洞导向代码切片、ERC-721知识库和约束DeepSeek分析的NFT智能合约漏洞检测方法，在450个样本上达到97.1%的正标签率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态分析器难以处理应用特定逻辑，而大语言模型分析可能被不相关代码干扰或产生不一致输出，亟需一种结合二者优势的精确漏洞检测方法。

Method: 使用正则表达式定位重入、整数溢出、时间戳依赖候选语句；通过结构感知的上下文窗口算法提取带行号的代码切片；DeepSeek基于显式决策规则和固定输出模式分析切片，结合ERC-721知识库进行约束。

Result: 完整配置下450个NFT合约样本的正标签率为97.1%；移除知识库后降至87.11%；无切片且无知识库时降至73.78%。

Conclusion: 聚焦的代码上下文和领域约束对检测器的输出有实质性影响，验证了该方法在NFT合约漏洞检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Ethereum non-fungible tokens (NFTs) implement ownership, transfer, authorization, and metadata operations through smart contracts, making contract vulnerabilities a direct risk to digital assets. Existing static analyzers provide efficient rule-based screening but can struggle with application-specific logic, whereas unconstrained large language model analysis may be distracted by irrelevant code or produce inconsistent outputs. We present a vulnerability-detection method that combines vulnerability-focused code slicing, an ERC-721-oriented knowledge base, and constrained DeepSeek analysis. Regular-expression patterns locate candidate statements for reentrancy, integer overflow or underflow, and timestamp dependence. A structure-aware context-window algorithm then extracts line-numbered code slices. DeepSeek analyzes each slice using explicit decision rules and a fixed output schema, and the resulting records support automated batch processing. On 450 NFT contract samples, the full configuration produced 437 positive labels, corresponding to a reported positive-label rate of 97.1%. Removing the external knowledge base reduced this rate to 87.11%, while analyzing complete contracts without the knowledge base reduced it to 73.78%. These results indicate that focused code context and domain constraints materially affect the detector's reported output.

</details>


### [119] [Practical Graph Optimisation and AI-Driven Models for Active Directory Security Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22009)
*Huy Q. Ngo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本论文针对微软Active Directory攻击图的三大实际挑战（动态性、防御手段单一、管理员反馈缺失），提出了基于博弈论和优化的决策模型，包括蜜罐部署、时域防御策略、自适应优先级排序及端到端自适应边缘移除策略，并证明了这些问题的计算复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有攻击图加固方案存在三大局限：1）假设图是静态的，而实际AD环境高度动态；2）仅关注撤销漏洞（边缘移除）这种被动防御，缺乏主动防御机制研究；3）未将系统管理员的可行性反馈纳入优先级排序流程，导致部分修复措施无法实施。

Method: 提出四种基于博弈论和优化的决策模型：1）蜜罐/诱饵放置模型（最小化最短路径数和域管理员可达节点数）；2）考虑AD图动态/时间特性的防御策略（最大化最坏情况下的入侵响应时间）；3）自适应优先级排序模型（向IT管理员查询高风险攻击路径）；4）端到端自适应优先级排序模型（学习管理员决策，泛化到风险特征相似的边缘，最小化审批工作量）。

Result: 证明了所提出模型背后的所有问题在计算上都是棘手的（computationally intractable）。

Conclusion: 本文针对AD攻击图加固的三大实际挑战，提出了多种博弈论与优化模型，涵盖蜜罐部署、时域防御、管理员反馈集成等方向，为实际AD环境的安全加固提供了理论框架，尽管其计算复杂性高，但为后续近似算法和实际部署指明了方向。

Abstract: Microsoft's Active Directory (AD) is a directory service that enables the IT admin to manage security permissions and control access within a Windows domain network. As a core management system in many of organisation, AD has become a primary target for adversaries. While many solutions for hardening attack graphs exist, these efforts fall short in addressing several key practical challenges specific to the AD attack graph. First, existing models often assume the graph is static, whereas a real-world AD environment is highly dynamic. Second, most proposed solutions are limited to the defensive measure of revoking vulnerabilities (edge removal), while more active defence mechanisms are largely unstudied. Third, because not all remediations are implementable, a practical end-to-end model must incorporate system admin feedback into the prioritisation process. This thesis aims to address these limitations by studying and proposing a number of game-theoretic and optimisation-based decision-making models. First, we propose a honeypot/decoy placement model based on the principle of minimising the number of shortest paths and the number of Domain Admin-reachable nodes. Second, building on this model, we introduce a defence strategy that considers the dynamic/temporal nature of the AD graph, where the objective is to find the location to deploy decoys that maximises the worst-case incident response time. Third, we introduce an adaptive prioritisation model that queries each high-risk attack path to the IT administrator for mediation. Finally, we introduce an end-to-end adaptive prioritisation model that minimises the approval effort of the system admin by finding a general adaptive edge-removal policy that generalises the system admin's decisions to edges with similar risk features. We show that the problems underlying all of the contributed models are computationally intractable.

</details>


### [120] [Agent Security Needs Redefinition through a Holistic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22024)
*Vincent Siu,Jingxuan He,Kyle Montgomery,Zhun Wang,Chenguang Wang,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证智能体安全本质上是上下文问题而非内容问题，提出基于来源授权、任务对齐、行为对齐和数据隔离四属性的上下文安全框架，并指出现有基于内容的安全评估存在结构性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全研究将安全视为动作内容问题（如指令是否看起来恶意），但相同指令（如"删除用户数据"）在不同上下文中意义截然不同，内容本身无法区分善意管理操作与恶意攻击。作者认为这种基于内容的框架系统性误定义了智能体安全问题。

Method: (1) 论证当前基于内容的安全框架的结构性问题，通过AgentDojo和WASP基准测试证明所有注入任务中的动作与合法工作流中的动作不可区分。 (2) 提出包含四个必须联合成立且持续评估的属性的上下文安全框架：来源授权、任务对齐、行为对齐、数据隔离。 (3) 重新组织现有防御方法，将其映射到实际近似保护的属性上。

Result: 间接提示注入被重新定义为来源授权违规；快照基准测试在结构上无法评估数据隔离；现有防御被围绕实际保护的属性重新组织。上下文重构改变了哪些防御是连贯的、哪些评估有实际价值、以及哪些攻击模式能被评估发现。

Conclusion: 智能体安全应从基于内容的评估转向上下文安全框架，通过持续评估四个安全属性来正确识别安全风险，而非仅依赖动作内容判断恶意性。

Abstract: Agent security is widely treated as a question about action content. Defenses ask whether an instruction looks malicious. Benchmarks ask whether an agent performs a harmful sounding action. \textbf{We argue that agent security is fundamentally a contextual problem, and that the current content based framing systematically misdefines it.} A command to ``delete user data'' might be a routine administrative request or a prompt injection attacking production systems, and the content alone cannot distinguish the two. Authorization context can. Across every injection task in AgentDojo and WASP, the same action is one an authenticated user would plausibly request in a routine workflow, which makes the conflation a structural property of evaluating security through content.
  We operationalize contextual security through four properties that must hold jointly and be evaluated continuously across the agent's trajectory. Source Authorization asks who issued the command. Task Alignment specifies the agent's authorized objective. Action Alignment evaluates whether each action serves that objective. Data Isolation governs information flows across privilege boundaries. Under this reframing, indirect prompt injection becomes a Source Authorization violation. Snapshot benchmarks are structurally incapable of evaluating Data Isolation. Existing defenses are reorganized around the property they actually approximate. The contextual reframing changes which defenses are coherent, which evaluations measure something useful, and which attack patterns evaluation can see at all.

</details>


### [121] [BioZKFHE: Scalable Encrypted Biometric Identification via Verifiable Homomorphic Similarity Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22065)
*Rundong Xin,Taotao Wang,Xiaoxiao Wu,Weizhi Meng,Shengli Zhang,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BioZKFHE提出了一种结合BGV同态加密与委员会辅助证明打开/解密及智能合约验证的框架，通过Single-Coefficient Multi-Value (SCMV)打包和Parallelizable and Verifiable Similarity Computation (PVSC)方案，在可证明安全的前提下实现了大规模加密生物特征识别的高效性、可验证性和近无损精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于FHE的生物识别系统主要关注机密性，但在实际应用中缺乏对加密相似性输出正确性的可验证性，主要瓶颈在于密文1:N匹配层的旋转和带宽开销大，以及重复同态相似性迹的证明成本高。

Method: 提出SCMV打包（基于基T扩展将多个量化嵌入值绑定到每个明文项中，减少密文存储并避免旋转密集型加密1:N匹配）和PVSC方案（利用BGV的Double-CRT执行结构将块状相似性迹分解为并行证明实例，在结果释放前打开和检查）。结合委员会辅助证明打开/解密与智能合约验证。

Result: 在FaceNet和MobileFaceNet上实现近无损生物识别精度，密文存储减少达67%，端到端证明验证运行时间在1万至4万模板规模下约为22-44秒。

Conclusion: BioZKFHE在标准格假设和显式委员会/验证者假设下，同时实现了可恢复性、噪声控制、机密性、加密输出完整性和最终结果完整性，为大规模外包生物特征识别提供了实用的可验证同态相似性评估方案。

Abstract: Large-scale biometric identification in outsourced settings requires two properties simultaneously: biometric templates and queries must remain protected during computation, and the encrypted similarity outputs produced by an untrusted compute node must be verifiably correct before any application result is released. Existing FHE-based biometric systems primarily address confidentiality, while practical verifiability introduces two bottlenecks in the underlying encrypted 1:N matching layer: rotation- and bandwidth-heavy similarity evaluation and the high cost of proving repeated homomorphic similarity traces. We present BioZKFHE, a framework for scalable encrypted biometric identification via verifiable homomorphic similarity evaluation that combines BGV homomorphic computation with committee-mediated proof opening/decryption and smart-contract verification of opened proof batches. To reduce encrypted storage and avoid rotation-heavy encrypted 1:N matching, we propose Single-Coefficient Multi-Value (SCMV) packing, which binds multiple quantized embedding values into each plaintext entry through base-T expansion. To make proof generation practical, we propose Parallelizable and Verifiable Similarity Computation (PVSC), which exploits the Double-CRT execution structure of BGV to decompose each blockwise similarity trace into parallel proof instances that are opened and checked before result release. Under standard lattice assumptions and explicit committee/verifier assumptions, we analyze recoverability, noise growth, confidentiality, encrypted-output integrity, and finalized-result integrity. Experiments on FaceNet and MobileFaceNet show near-lossless biometric utility, up to 67 percent encrypted-storage reduction, and about 22 to 44 seconds end-to-end proof-verified runtime for 10k to 40k templates.

</details>


### [122] [PoCEvolve: Generating Proof-of-Concept Exploits from Security Patches with Vulnerability-Aware Prompt Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22076)
*Duc Manh Tran,Ratnadira Widyasari,Ivana Clairine Irsan,Huihui Huang,Ting Zhang,Shar Lwin Khin,Ouh Eng Lieh,Hong Jin Kang,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PoCEvolve，一种直接从漏洞修复提交生成PoC漏洞利用的框架。针对漏洞详细信息在修复提交后长时间延迟公开的问题，PoCEvolve通过漏洞感知的提示进化框架，利用推断的漏洞API和代码覆盖信息指导提示进化，在SecBench.VFC.js上达到58.4%的PoC生成成功率（比PoCGen提高20.7%），使用Qwen3.7-Plus模型可达85.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞详细信息通常在修复提交之后很久才公开，导致修复窗口期内防御者无法评估暴露风险、确定优先级和验证修复效果，而攻击者可通过逆向工程利用已公开的补丁。

Method: 提出PoCEvolve框架，从漏洞修复提交直接合成PoC。该方法通过评估不同维度漏洞相关上下文（如推断的漏洞API和代码覆盖信息）的有效性，指导提示进化的方向，从失败的生成尝试中学习并优化后续提示。

Result: 在SecBench.VFC.js基准上，PoCEvolve的PoC生成成功率为58.4%，相对PoCGen提升20.7%，比GPT-4o-mini LLM基线提升200.0%；使用Qwen3.7-Plus模型成功率提升至85.3%；有详细漏洞报告时成功率为71.7%，比PoCGen提升11.1%。

Conclusion: PoCEvolve能有效缓解漏洞修复提交与详细报告公开之间的信息缺口，显著优于现有方法，且性能随模型升级进一步提升。

Abstract: Ideally, the detailed information about a vulnerability should be made available together with the fixing commit. In practice, however, such details often become available only long after the commit, even when a CVE has already been published. During this window, the patch is already public, so attackers can reverse-engineer it, yet defenders lack the details needed to assess exposure, prioritize, and validate the fix. Executable evidence, such as a proof-of-concept (PoC) exploit, could fill this gap. Prior work has automated PoC generation, but the state-of-the-art approach, PoCGen, assumes that a detailed vulnerability report is already available, which is precisely what is missing during this window. In this paper, we first present an empirical study quantifying the long delay between the fixing commit and the availability of a detailed vulnerability report. We then introduce PoCEvolve, a vulnerability-aware prompt-evolution framework that generates PoCs directly from vulnerability-fixing commits. Given a vulnerability-fixing commit, PoCEvolve synthesizes a corresponding PoC exploit. To learn from unsuccessful generation attempts, PoCEvolve assesses the usefulness of different dimensions of vulnerability-related context, including the inferred vulnerable API and code-coverage information. These assessments guide prompt evolution towards more effective exploit-generation prompts. We evaluate PoCEvolve on SecBench.VFC.js, where PoCEvolve achieves a PoC generation success rate of 58.4%, corresponding to relative improvements of 20.7% over PoCGen and 200.0% over the LLM baseline with GPT-4o-mini. With a recent model, Qwen3.7-Plus, PoCEvolve achieves a higher success rate of 85.3%. When detailed vulnerability reports are available, PoCEvolve achieves a success rate of 71.7%, improving over PoCGen by 11.1%.

</details>


### [123] [Transforming Keystroke Noise to Text: Self-Supervised Acoustic Eavesdropping Attacks on Keyboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22094)
*Atsunori Okada,Akira Ito,Rei Ueno,Yuichi Hayashi,Naofumi Homma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种自监督声学窃听攻击方法，仅通过键盘敲击声重建键入文本，无需目标设备的标注数据。方法结合无监督声学聚类、Transformer语言模型推理和迭代自训练，在智能手机近距离录制下仅需100-150次击键即可达到99%以上的重建准确率，在多种现实场景（3米距离、穿墙窃听、在线会议背景噪声）下也实现了90%以上的高准确率，揭示了键盘声学侧信道攻击的实际隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于键盘敲击声的文本重建方法多依赖目标设备的标注数据，限制了实际场景中的可用性；且缺乏对无监督条件下、低数据量场景的深入研究。作者旨在揭示一种无需目标设备标注数据即可实现高精度声学窃听攻击的可行性，强调该隐私风险被低估。

Method: 1) 无监督声学聚类：对键盘敲击声进行特征提取和聚类，获得粗略的声学-字符映射；2) Transformer语言模型推理：利用语言模型的上下文信息纠正聚类产生的歧义；3) 迭代自训练：反复优化聚类和语言模型之间的相互约束，逐步提升字符推断的稳定性。

Result: 1) 近距离智能手机录制下，100-150次击键即可实现99%以上的重建准确率，显著优于先前的无监督基线方法；2) 在多种笔记本电脑平台上均表现鲁棒；3) 3米远的桌面录制、穿墙接触式麦克风窃听、在线会议背景键盘噪声等场景下，150-250次击键实现超90%的高准确率。

Conclusion: 纯音频设置下，仅凭有限的击键次数即可实现高精度的文本重建，且无需设备特定的标注数据。该攻击在物理空间和在线会议中均具备现实可行性，表明键盘敲击声作为侧信道带来了被低估的隐私威胁，需引起重视。

Abstract: We present a self-supervised acoustic eavesdropping attack that reconstructs typed text solely from keystroke sounds, without requiring labeled data for the target device. The proposed attack enables stealthy eavesdropping in two real-world scenarios-physical spaces (public and semi-public) and online meetings. Our method combines unsupervised acoustic clustering with Transformer-based language model inference and iterative self-training, enabling stable character inference under highly uncertain acoustic-to-character mappings. We demonstrate that the proposed method achieves over 99% reconstruction accuracy with only 100-150 observed keystrokes under a close-proximity recording setup using a smartphone placed near the target device, significantly outperforming prior unsupervised baselines in low-data regimes. We further evaluate robustness across multiple laptop platforms and in realistic acquisition channels, including distance recording from approximately 3 meters away on the same desk, through-the-wall eavesdropping with a contact microphone, and background keyboard noise in online conferencing systems. Across these scenarios, the proposed method achieves high reconstruction accuracy (often exceeding 90%) with approximately 150-250 observed keystrokes. These results indicate that accurate text reconstruction from keystroke sounds is feasible in practice under an audio-only setting, even with limited observed keystrokes and without requiring device-specific labeled data, highlighting a realistic and previously underestimated privacy risk.

</details>


### [124] [DeFiScreener: Efficient DeFi Attack Pre-screening in Smart Contracts via Historical Case Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22184)
*Rui Cao,Shaojing Fan,Zhimei Sui,Liming Fang,Ziqi Yang,Yingying Jiao,Zhenguang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DeFiScreener，首个基于历史攻击案例的DeFi攻击预筛选框架，利用大语言模型和函数调用树进行双重筛选，在207个真实攻击事件中达到98.55%召回率和84.30%精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeFi检测工具通常仅覆盖特定攻击类型，检测覆盖率严重受限。作者认为有效的解决方式是从海量智能合约函数和调用序列中预筛选易受攻击的实例。

Method: 基于"危险时间不对称性"现象，提出DeFiScreener框架：(1)为目标项目构建函数调用树(FCT)；(2)利用大语言模型生成每个函数的语义嵌入，融合程序结构和函数意图；(3)函数级筛选：将函数嵌入与历史攻击模式库匹配；(4)序列级筛选：提出攻击导向蒙特卡洛树搜索(APO-MCTS)高效探索FCT并筛选易受攻击的调用序列；(5)将候选结果传递给LLM进行进一步安全分析。

Result: 在包含207个真实DeFi攻击事件的数据集上，DeFiScreener实现了98.55%的召回率和84.30%的精确率。

Conclusion: DeFiScreener作为首个自动化DeFi攻击预筛选框架，通过结合历史攻击模式、LLM语义分析和双重筛选机制，有效解决了现有工具检测覆盖率不足的问题，实现了高召回率和高精确率的攻击预筛选。

Abstract: Blockchain and its killer applications, particularly decentralized finance (DeFi), are gaining widespread adoption, with over 5,200 DeFi projects deployed on mainstream blockchains as of January 2026. At the same time, security risks in DeFi are becoming increasingly serious. However, existing DeFi detection tools usually cover only specific attack types, exhibiting severely limited detection coverage.
  In this paper, we argue that an effective way to address this gap is to pre-screen vulnerable instances from large volumes of smart contract functions and call sequences. This is motivated by a key phenomenon we term "perilous temporal asymmetry". Inspired by this, we propose DeFiScreener, the first automated pre-screening framework for DeFi attacks that uses historical exploit cases to identify potentially vulnerable functions and call sequences. Given the full source code of a target project, DeFiScreener builds Function Call Trees (FCTs) and generates semantic embeddings for each function using a large language model (LLM), allowing both program structure and function intent to be analyzed together. It then applies a dual-level screening process. At the function level, function embeddings are matched against an Attack Pattern Library of historically exploited functions. At the sequence level, the proposed Attack Pattern Oriented Monte Carlo Tree Search (APO-MCTS) efficiently explores the FCTs and screens vulnerable call sequences. The identified candidates are ultimately passed to an LLM for further interpretive and security analysis.
  We empirically evaluate the DeFiScreener over datasets comprising 207 real-world DeFi attack incidents. Experimental results demonstrate that DeFiScreener achieves a remarkable 98.55% recall and 84.30% precision in attack pre-screening.

</details>


### [125] [trasgoDP: An Open Source Framework for Releasing Noised Tabular Microdata under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22230)
*Judith Sáinz-Pardo Díaz,Álvaro López García*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: trasgoDP是一个模块化、开源、易用的Python框架，用于在ε-局部差分隐私和地理不可区分性保证下发布表格微数据和位置数据，支持多机制、多数据类型和隐私-效用权衡的系统探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私在聚合数据上已被广泛研究，但在行级微数据发布方面缺乏可重用的软件工具，这一差距在度量隐私和位置数据领域更为显著。

Method: 实现了局部差分隐私机制（Laplace、Gaussian、Exponential、Randomized Response）用于数值和分类属性，地理不可区分性机制用于位置数据，以及一套效用度量指标（含新颖的相关性损失度量）。

Result: 提供了一个可复现、开源的工具框架，支持在不同机制、数据类型和ε值下系统性评估隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 旨在为研究社区提供评估表格和位置数据发布方法的可复现开源基线，推动局部差分隐私在实际数据科学工作流中的集成应用。

Abstract: trasgoDP is a modular, open-source, and easy-to-use Python framework for releasing tabular microdata under ε-local differential privacy guarantees, as well as location data under geo-indistinguishability assumptions, designed to be installed and integrated within standard data science workflows. The software enables systematic exploration of privacy-utility trade-offs across multiple mechanisms, data types, and ε values. While differential privacy has been extensively studied for aggregate data, its application to row-wise microdata release remains underexploited in terms of reusable software tools, a gap that is even more pronounced in the case of metric privacy and location-based data. trasgoDP implements local-DP mechanisms for numerical and categorical attributes (Laplace, Gaussian, Exponential, and Randomized Response), a geo-indistinguishability mechanism for location data, and a set of utility metrics, including a novel correlation-loss measure, to quantify information loss as a function of the allocated privacy budget. The objective of this work is to provide the research community with a reproducible, open-source baseline for evaluating tabular and location-based data publication methodologies under formal local differential privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [126] [Kalyna Block Cipher: From Design Space Exploration to ASIC Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22269)
*Carlos Gewehr,Levent Aksoy,Mariia Rodinko,Roman Oliynykov,Samuel Pagliarini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出Kalyna分组密码的硬件加速器实现，探索了多种设计架构（加密/解密/统一模式），并集成了抗侧信道和故障注入攻击的防护措施，最终在65nm ASIC芯片上验证了不同设计方案在面积、延迟和能耗之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kalyna是乌克兰国家标准分组密码，虽有软件实现，但缺乏硬件高效的加速器设计，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 探索多种设计架构（加密、解密、统一加解密），提出硬件缩减技术及低面积/低延迟/低能耗的替代设计；集成隐藏、掩码、复制等抗SCA和FI攻击的防护措施，并在65nm ASIC上实现验证。

Result: 通过ASIC芯片测量验证了各设计的功能性；表明不同需求下需要探索设计搜索空间；提出的架构能通过操作随机化隐藏功率SCA泄漏，时序复制可缓解FI攻击。

Conclusion: 该工作为Kalyna密码提供了首个硬件加速器方案，实现了面积、延迟、能耗与安全性（抗SCA/FI）之间的设计空间探索，并通过实际芯片验证了可行性。

Abstract: The Kalyna block cipher is a Ukrainian cryptography standard, selected through a national competition held between 2007 and 2010 and approved in 2015. Although its software implementations have been introduced, hardware-efficient implementations of the algorithm, i.e., accelerators, do not exist. In this paper, we explore various design architectures to implement its encryption, decryption, and unified encryption/decryption functions, considering the trade-off between area and latency. We present hardware reduction techniques and introduce alternative designs with low area, latency, and energy consumption, targeting an application-specific integrated circuit (ASIC). We present hardware-efficient designs that include countermeasures against side-channel analysis (SCA) and fault injection (FI) attacks, such as hiding, masking, and duplication techniques. We validate these implementations in a 65\;nm ASIC chip. Experimental results confirm the need for alternative designs that explore the design search space for different requirements. The proposed architectures enable hiding the power SCA leakage by randomizing the execution of operations, and the temporal duplication in designs with countermeasures against the SCA attacks can mitigate the FI attacks. The functionality of the ASIC test chip, including various Kalyna designs, is validated through measurements.

</details>


### [127] [A Maximum Entropy Implementation of Differential Privacy Under Linear Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22450)
*Ryan Lafferty,Anindya Roy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在聚合约束（不变性）下实现高熵差分隐私的方法，保证了约束以概率1或接近1被满足，推导了相应的隐私保证，并部分解决了相关矩阵零空间的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的差分隐私实现（如独立高斯或拉普拉斯噪声添加）在处理聚合线性查询时，无法满足某些必须保留的约束（如宪法规定的州总数不能扰动）。这些约束（称为不变性）要求噪声不再是独立的，因此需要重新评估隐私保证。

Method: 提出一种高熵差分隐私实现方法，在聚合约束下以概率1或以指数级接近1的概率维持不变性；推导了该实现下的隐私保证；理论证明部分解决了关于相关矩阵零空间的开放问题；该方法也可推广到线性等式约束下的正态混合模型采样。

Result: 该方法能够在维持聚合约束不变性的同时提供差分隐私保障，并部分解决了相关矩阵零空间的理论问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法不仅适用于需要满足特定约束的差分隐私应用（如美国十年一次的人口普查），还具有在正态混合模型采样等更广泛场景下的通用价值。

Abstract: Differential privacy is the standard for ensuring data privacy and is widely used in major data publications, including reporting results from the U.S. decennial census. Common implementation of differential privacy uses independent Gaussian or Laplace noise addition to the database. However, there could be aggregate (linear) queries to the database that are excluded from the privacy budget, for example, state totals that can not be perturbed due to constitutional mandates. Any implementation of a differential privacy is required to honor these constraints, also referred to as invariants. Under aggregation constraints, the noise vector is no longer independent and the traditional differential privacy guarantees have to be re-evaluated. We propose a high entropy differential privacy implementation that maintains the aggregation invariants with probability one or exponentially close to one and derive the privacy guarantees for the implementation under the invariants. The theoretical proof covers a partial solution to an open question about the null space of correlation matrices. Moreover, the methodology has general use in the context of sampling from normal mixture models under linear equality constraints.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Prior laundering: learned priors with inherited, undetectable overconfidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21721)
*Ali Siahkoohi,Sina Alemohammad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 利用学习生成先验处理贝叶斯逆问题时，若先验仅由历史重建档案（而非真实数据）训练得到，会导致后验不确定性在数据无信息区域完全继承档案的“信念”而非数据本身的信息，即使通过自洽性检查也无法发现，且这种继承等价于将旧正则化器执行一步期望最大化，可能产生过度自信。实验表明，扩散先验和归一化流在盲子空间上的覆盖均不如真实数据训练的控制组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在地震和医学成像等场景中，真实“真值”难以获取，实践中只能使用历史重建档案来训练生成先验（即“先验洗白”）。然而这种方法对后验不确定性的影响尚不清楚，亟需理论分析。

Method: 理论推导证明：在数据无信息的方向上，后验会退化为先验，不确定性反映的是档案的信念而非数据；该信念等价于将旧正则化器执行一步期望最大化。使用扩散先验和归一化流在盲子空间上进行覆盖率的数值实验验证。

Result: 1）档案训练的先验在数据无法分辨的方向上产生过度自信的不确定性；2）单张最佳档案会使盲置信区间宽度坍缩至零；3）扩散先验和归一化流在盲子空间上的覆盖均低于真实数据训练的控制组。

Conclusion: 建议在实践中明确报告哪些方向被测量数据分辨，从而区分数据支持的信度与通过流程继承的信念。

Abstract: Learned generative priors are increasingly used for ill-posed Bayesian inverse problems, their posterior uncertainty treated as earned from data. But training one requires truths, scarce in seismic and medical imaging, so the recourse is an archive of legacy reconstructions---prior laundering. Where the measurements are uninformative the posterior reverts to the prior, so the uncertainty reported there is the archive's, not the data's, and nothing in deployment reveals it: truths differing only on those directions induce identical data laws, and self-consistency checks such as simulation-based calibration pass whatever the prior believes. That belief has an exact source: averaging the legacy posterior over the measurements yields the old regularizer advanced a single expectation--maximization step---improved where the data resolve, frozen where they cannot. It is overconfident wherever the inherited belief is tighter than the truth. A single-best archive is worse, collapsing the blind credible interval to zero width. Deployed, a diffusion prior fit to the archive under-covers the operator's blind subspace, unlike a truth-trained control, and a normalizing flow does the same on a nonlinear groundwater operator. We recommend reporting which directions the measurements resolve, separating confidence the data support from belief inherited through the pipeline.

</details>


### [129] [Simulation-Based Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21843)
*Xinwei Shen,Diana Cai,Cheng Zhang,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了仿真经验贝叶斯（SBEB），将非参数经验贝叶斯与基于仿真的推断相结合，解决了似然函数仅通过仿真器可得时的经验贝叶斯推断问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典经验贝叶斯要求似然函数 p(x|z) 可解析求解，但在许多科学应用中，似然函数只能通过仿真器获取，这限制了经验贝叶斯在这些场景下的应用。

Method: SBEB 利用观测数据、仿真器样本和摊销推理网络，在无需显式密度的情况下计算经验贝叶斯估计，并通过迭代将拟合的经验贝叶斯先验向总体先验逼近。

Result: 在多个科学仿真器和真实世界数据上，SBEB 相比固定先验的基于仿真的推断方法在准确率上有所提升。

Conclusion: SBEB 有效扩展了经验贝叶斯到隐式似然场景，为科学仿真中的推断问题提供了更精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Empirical Bayes (EB) performs simultaneous inference across many related latent variables. Classical EB assumes that the likelihood p(x | z) is tractable. In many scientific applications, however, the likelihood is available only through a simulator. This paper develops EB for such implicit likelihoods. We introduce simulation-based empirical Bayes (SBEB), which connects nonparametric EB to simulation-based inference (SBI). SBEB computes EB estimates without an explicit density by using the observed data, simulator samples, and an amortized inference network. SBEB iteratively refines the fitted EB prior toward the population prior. With several scientific simulators and real-world data, we demonstrate that SBEB improves accuracy over SBI with a fixed prior.

</details>


### [130] [Efficient Online LLM Watermark Detection via Rao-Blackwellized E-Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21958)
*Lu Luo,Dandan Mo,Chengdong Xu,Ting Li,Jinhan Xie,Huiqiong Li,Niansheng Tang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Rao-Blackwellized e过程的在线水印检测框架，支持任意时点有效推断和流式生成中的早期停止，适用于Gumbel-max水印，并在理论上证明了任意时点第一类错误控制和水印下的渐近对数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型文本水印检测方法多为固定视界程序，无法在流式生成中实现有效的早期停止，缺乏在线检测的灵活性。

Method: 基于Rao-Blackwellized e过程构建在线水印检测框架，支持递归的token级证据更新；将Gumbel-max水印的token级依赖检验转化为枢轴量驱动的序贯检验问题，并推导出显式的零分布。

Result: 理论证明了任意时点第一类错误控制（在可选停止下成立）以及水印下的正渐近对数增长率；仿真和真实LLM文本实验验证了高效的在线检测性能。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了严格的任意时点保证下的在线水印检测，为流式场景提供了可靠高效的解决方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed, reliable and efficient mechanisms for distinguishing AI-generated text from human-written content have become essential. Statistical watermarking has emerged as a promising solution, yet most existing methods are typically fixed-horizon procedures, precluding valid early stopping in streaming generation. In this paper, we develop an efficient online watermark detection framework with anytime-valid inference based on Rao-Blackwellized e-processes, enabling recursive token-level evidence updates without storing the full history. In particular, we instantiate the framework for the Gumbel-max watermark and reduce the original token-level dependence testing problem to a pivot-induced sequential testing problem with an explicit null distribution. Theoretically, we prove anytime-valid Type I error control under arbitrary optional stopping and establish positive asymptotic log-growth under watermarking, implying consistency of the proposed stopping rules. Simulations and experiments on real LLM-generated text demonstrate efficient online detection with rigorous anytime-valid guarantees.

</details>


### [131] [Convergence analysis of a family of Zermelo-type iterations for the Bradley--Terry model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22221)
*Ruijian Han,Ding Lu,Yiming Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Zermelo型固定点迭代算法在Bradley-Terry模型中的局部收敛性，推导了同步和异步更新下的收敛因子闭合表达式，通过谱分析揭示了参数α对收敛速度的影响机制。结果表明：异步更新下α=0最优且单调收敛，而同步更新在α<1时可能发散。加速的关键不仅是选择α=0，更在于使用异步更新策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Newman提出的Zermelo型迭代算法家族中，α=0的经验收敛速度显著优于经典Zermelo算法(α=1)，但其加速机制缺乏理论解释。本文旨在从局部收敛分析角度提供理论洞见，揭示加速现象背后的原理。

Method: 通过谱分析推导同步和异步更新下算法的Jacobian矩阵，获得局部收敛因子的闭合表达式；在群体BT模型和一致有序二分比较图假设下，分析收敛因子对α的依赖关系（拟凸性/单调性），并建立渐进近似结果以验证实践相关性。

Result: 同步更新下α<1可能不收敛，局部收敛因子在群体BT模型中关于α拟凸；异步更新下始终局部收敛，在一致有序二分比较图的群体BT模型中收敛因子关于α单调递增，证明α=0最优。数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: α=0加速效应不仅源于参数选择，更关键的是异步更新策略的使用。该分析补充了现有收敛性结果，为实际应用中选择最优参数和更新策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Zermelo's algorithm is a classical method for computing the maximum likelihood estimator in the Bradley--Terry (BT) model, but its convergence can be slow in practice. To accelerate computation, Newman introduced a family of Zermelo-type fixed-point iterations parameterized by $α$, with Zermelo's algorithm recovered at $α=1$. Empirical evidence suggests that the choice $α=0$ often converges substantially faster, making it a promising alternative, yet the mechanism underlying this acceleration remains elusive. This paper provides theoretical insight into this phenomenon through a systematic local convergence analysis. We derive closed-form expressions for local convergence factors under synchronous and asynchronous updates and analyze their dependence on $α$ via spectral analysis of the associated Jacobian matrices. For synchronous updates, we show that the algorithm may fail to converge when $α<1$, and its local convergence factor is quasi-convex in $α$ under the population BT model. In contrast, asynchronous updates are always locally convergent, and their local convergence factor is provably monotonically increasing in $α$ under the population BT model of consistently ordered bipartite comparison graphs, establishing the optimality of $α=0$ in this setting. We further establish asymptotic approximation results for the population convergence factors under the BT model, justifying their practical relevance. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets confirm the theory. Our analysis complements existing convergence results and shows that the acceleration of $α=0$ arises not only from the parameter choice but, more importantly, from the use of asynchronous updates.

</details>


### [132] [Variational Low-rank Tensor Decomposition for Multisubject Spatiotemporal Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22262)
*Laura M. Montaldo,Ricardo A. Borsoi,Sebastian Miron,Tulay Adali*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空变分张量分解（ST-VTD）框架，通过结合LL1分解启发的低秩空间先验和基于LSTM的自适应时间先验，并采用摊销变分推理与组独立成分分析热启动策略，在合成fMRI数据上显著提升了潜因子恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像等多主体时空数据中，空间和时间模式存在丰富的跨主体变异性，现有的矩阵和张量分解方法依赖固定的多线性结构或耦合方案，灵活性有限，难以捕捉复杂变异。

Method: 提出ST-VTD框架：1) 张量分解生成模型结合结构化先验；2) 空间因子采用LL1分解启发的低秩正则化；3) 时间因子采用基于LSTM的自适应先验；4) 通过展开优化算法迭代实现摊销变分后验推理；5) 采用组独立成分分析热启动策略提升优化性能。

Result: 在逼真的合成fMRI数据集上，ST-VTD相比经典和概率性分解基准方法，显著提升了潜因子的恢复效果。

Conclusion: ST-VTD框架有效建模了多主体时空数据中的共享与个体特异性结构，为神经影像数据分析提供了一种灵活且可解释的分解方法。

Abstract: Modeling shared and subject-specific structure in multisubject spatiotemporal data remains challenging, particularly in neuroimaging, where both spatial and temporal patterns exhibit rich variability across subjects. Existing matrix and tensor decompositions provide interpretable factorizations, but rely on fixed multilinear structures or coupling schemes that may limit their flexibility in capturing complex variability. In this work, we introduce a spatiotemporal variational tensor decomposition (ST-VTD) framework that combines a tensor factorization generative model with structured priors to jointly represent spatial maps and temporal dynamics. Spatial factors are regularized to promote a low-rank structure inspired by the LL1 decomposition, while temporal factors are modeled using a learned Long short-term memory (LSTM)-based prior, enabling flexible and adaptive dynamics. Posterior inference is performed using an amortized variational formulation by unrolling iterations of an optimization algorithm, leading to an interpretable and parameter-efficient architecture. The proposed inference framework employs a warm-start strategy based on group independent component analysis, which we found to improve optimization performance. Experiments on a realistic synthetic functional MRI (fMRI) dataset demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly improves latent factor recovery compared with representative classical and probabilistic decomposition benchmarks.

</details>


### [133] [General Value Functions for Remaining Useful Life and Failure-Mode Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22268)
*Hao Yan,Ali Sarabi,Qing Zou,Boyang Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将预测性维护中的剩余寿命预测和故障模式分类形式化为向量广义价值函数（GVF）预测问题，使用多步时序差分估计器TD(n,λ)替代传统的固定窗口监督学习，从而利用碎片化、无身份标识的退化数据中的局部Bellman转移，在标签稀缺场景下提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定窗口监督学习依赖完整的终局标签且无法编码连续退化预测间的时间递归关系，当观测部分缺失或单元身份不可用时表现不佳。

Method: 将预测任务形式化为吸收退化过程上的向量GVF预测，RUL和故障模式概率作为时序一致的目标；采用多步时序差分估计器TD(n,λ)进行估计；理论上推导了向量GVF的Bellman不动点，刻画了线性投影TD极限及其与完整回报蒙特卡洛回归的关系。

Result: 在事件触发多模式仿真和NASA C-MAPSS标签稀缺拼接数据上，TD方法在RUL和故障模式预测上优于使用相同骨干网络的监督蒙特卡洛控制方法，尤其在完整标签稀缺时优势明显。

Conclusion: 碎片化、无身份标识的退化记录可以通过贡献局部Bellman转移参与训练，无需等待完整运行至故障标签；TD估计器在标签稀缺场景下具有更低的方差和更好的预测性能。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) prediction and failure-mode classification are central tasks in predictive maintenance. Many data-driven pipelines use fixed-window supervised learning with complete terminal labels; such routes do not naturally encode the temporal recursion linking successive degradation-state predictions when observations are partial or unit identities are unavailable. We formulate prognostics as vector General Value Function (GVF) prediction on an absorbing degradation process, treating RUL and failure-mode probabilities as temporally consistent targets rather than independent window-level labels, and estimate them with a multi-step temporal-difference estimator, TD($n,λ$). Supporting theory identifies the Bellman fixed point of the vector GVFs, characterizes the linear projected-TD limit and its relation to complete-return Monte Carlo regression under realizability, and explains when bootstrapped TD targets are less variable than Monte Carlo returns. On an event-triggered multimode simulation and NASA C-MAPSS label-scarce stitch data, TD improves RUL and failure-mode prediction relative to a supervised same-backbone Monte Carlo control, especially under scarce complete labels. Practically, fragmented, identity-free degradation records can contribute local Bellman transitions instead of being discarded until complete run-to-failure labels are available.

</details>


### [134] [Hopformer: Homogeneity-Pursuit Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22299)
*Wan Zhang,Qinjie Lin,Chan Lee,Weijian Li,Han Liu,Kai Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Hopformer框架，通过两阶段方式处理高维协变量下的多时间序列预测：第一阶段用稀疏模式聚合提取共同低方差趋势，第二阶段用LoRA微调Transformer建模残差复杂依赖，在多个基准上平均提升MASE 6.56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多时间序列预测面临统一共同时间模式与保留序列特异信息的核心挑战，现有方法难以同时兼顾两者。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段Sparsity Pattern Aggregation (SPA)方案提取包含协变量的共同低方差趋势作为同质化层；第二阶段LoRA微调Transformer对残差中的复杂依赖进行建模。

Result: 在合成和真实世界预测基准上，MASE平均提升6.56%，达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: SPA方案通过oracle不等式实现近乎最优的偏差-方差权衡，第二阶段在相依时间序列数据下具有泛化界保证，理论完备且实证有效。

Abstract: Forecasting multiple time-series with high-dimensional covariates presents a core challenge: unifying common temporal patterns while retaining meaningful series-specific information. We introduce Hopformer (Homogeneity-Pursuit Transformer), a two-stage framework that addresses this challenge. In the first stage, we perform a Sparsity Pattern Aggregation (SPA) scheme extracting a common low-variance trend that incorporates the covariates. This acts as a homogenization layer. In the second stage, a LoRA-fine-tuned Transformer models the remaining complex dependencies in the residual. Our method is theoretically grounded. We prove that SPA achieves a near-optimal bias-variance trade-off via an oracle inequality. We also provide generalization bounds for the second stage under dependent time series data. Hopformer sets a new state of the art, improving MASE by an average of 6.56% across synthetic and real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [135] [Learning Bidirectional Causal Interactions with Heteroscedastic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22313)
*Masahiro Tanaka*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出SEM-DNN，一种异方差神经联立方程估计器，利用条件协方差对角化实现无外部工具变量的结构交互系数识别，并通过蒙特卡洛实验和真实数据验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据中估计同期双向交互效应十分困难，因为每个结果变量都内生地依赖于另一个结果变量，而灵活的非参数回归只能捕捉约化形式的依赖关系，无法识别真实的因果结构参数。现有方法通常需要外部工具变量，但在许多实际场景中难以获得有效的工具变量。

Method: 提出SEM-DNN（异方差神经联立方程估计器）。识别策略：当结构冲击具有条件零均值、给定预定义协变量时条件不相关且具有非比例条件方差时，只有真实交互系数能使条件残差协方差矩阵在特征空间上对角化。估计方法：联合逼近非线性结构均值函数和特征依赖方差，使用包含联立系统雅可比矩阵的对角高斯拟似然函数。

Result: 建立了唯一识别性和正定局部曲率的轮廓总体准则；证明了在神经轮廓兼容条件下，即使网络参数化不唯一，神经准则也能继承这种曲率。蒙特卡洛实验表明，随着信息量增加，SEM-DNN比参数方法、核方法和分离方程神经网络方法更可靠地恢复结构效应，尽管计算成本更高。应用于即食麦片扫描数据，展示该方法可研究同期价格-销售反馈。

Conclusion: SEM-DNN在无需外部工具变量的条件下有效识别同期双向结构交互，具有因果解释意义，适用于非线性、高维复杂场景。

Abstract: Estimating contemporaneous bidirectional interactions from observational data is difficult because each outcome is endogenous to the other, while flexible regressions may capture only reduced-form dependence. This paper proposes SEM-DNN, a heteroscedastic neural simultaneous-equation estimator that learns reciprocal structural interactions without external instruments. Identification exploits conditional covariance diagonalization: when structural shocks have zero conditional means, are conditionally uncorrelated given predetermined covariates, and exhibit nonproportional conditional variances, only the true interaction coefficients diagonalize the conditional residual covariance across the feature space. The method jointly approximates nonlinear structural mean functions and feature-dependent variances using a diagonal Gaussian quasi-likelihood that incorporates the simultaneous-system Jacobian. We establish unique identification and positive-definite local curvature of the profiled population criterion and show that, under neural-profile compatibility conditions, the implemented neural criterion inherits this curvature despite nonunique network parameterizations. The coefficients admit a causal interpretation when the structural equations represent autonomous mechanisms that remain invariant under the relevant interventions. Monte Carlo experiments with nonlinear, high-dimensional nuisance functions and non-Gaussian shocks show that SEM-DNN recovers structural effects more reliably than parametric, kernel-based, and separate-equation neural alternatives as information increases, although at greater computational cost. An application to ready-to-eat cereal scanner data illustrates how the method can study contemporaneous price-sales feedback and assess identification strength, residual diagonalization, variance calibration, and optimization sensitivity.

</details>


### [136] [Learning Ergodic Dynamical Systems from a Finite Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22399)
*Oleksii Kachaiev,Silvia Villa,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究遍历随机动力系统单条有限轨迹的学习问题，针对离散时间自治随机系统定义的时间齐次马尔可夫过程，首先通过非线性最小二乘估计最优一步预测函数，并基于过程不变测度推导高概率保证，揭示了轨迹数据的非独立非同分布特性如何改变经典统计学习分析；随后将框架扩展至高阶系统和有限状态空间，最后证明相同的最小二乘和集中性论证可自然扩展至学习Koopman算子。方法结合了统计学习理论和马尔可夫链定量遍历理论，特别依赖于一致几何遍历马尔可夫链的Hilbert空间值加性泛函的集中不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计学习理论主要假设数据独立同分布，但实际中来自动力系统轨迹的数据具有非独立非同分布特性。本文旨在建立适用于遍历动力系统单条轨迹的统计学习理论框架，揭示数据依赖性和非平稳性对学习保证的影响。

Method: 结合统计学习理论与马尔可夫链定量遍历理论，利用一致几何遍历马尔可夫链的Hilbert空间值加性泛函的集中不等式，通过非线性最小二乘估计最优一步预测函数，并扩展至高阶系统、有限状态空间和Koopman算子学习。

Result: 推导了基于过程不变测度的高概率保证，明确了轨迹的非独立非同分布特性如何修改经典统计学习分析，并成功将框架扩展至更广泛的系统和算子学习场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效解决了遍历动力系统单轨迹学习的理论问题，为动力系统领域的数据驱动建模提供了统计学习理论基础，且方法具有可扩展性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning from a single finite trajectory of an ergodic stochastic dynamical system. More precisely, we study discrete-time autonomous stochastic systems defining time-homogeneous Markov processes. We first focus on estimating the optimal one-step prediction function by nonlinear least squares, and derive high-probability guarantees measured with respect to the invariant measure of the process. These results make explicit how the non-independent and non-identically distributed nature of trajectory data modifies the classical statistical learning analysis. We then extend the framework to higher-order systems and finite-state spaces. Finally, we show that the same least squares and concentration arguments naturally extend to learning Koopman operators. Our approach combines tools from statistical learning theory and quantitative ergodic theory for Markov chains. It relies, in particular, on a concentration inequality for Hilbert-space-valued additive functionals of uniformly geometrically ergodic Markov chains.

</details>


### [137] [Graph-Based Correlation Matrix Generation: A Convex Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22436)
*Ali Fakhar,K{é}vin Polisano,Ir{è}ne Gannaz,Sophie Achard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的凸优化框架，用于生成具有指定图结构稀疏模式的理论相关矩阵。该方法将初始矩阵投影到椭圆体上并满足半正定约束，通过控制非对角元素的均值分布，生成更符合真实数据的相关矩阵，并提供了理论解的存在性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法灵活控制相关矩阵非对角元素的分布，生成的相关矩阵难以真实反映实际数据的特征。本文旨在开发一种原则上可调谐的方法，生成具有指定图结构稀疏模式的相关矩阵，用于图模型推断统计方法的基准测试。

Method: 提出一个凸优化框架，将初始矩阵投影到椭圆体（elliptope）上并施加半正定约束。具体通过矩阵补全的方式，将缺失边对应的非对角元素固定为零，对角元素固定为一。该方法还允许控制非对角元素分布的均值，提供比现有方法更大的灵活性。

Result: 建立了在一般设置下和额外均值约束下解的存在性理论保证。仿真实验展示了生成矩阵在不同图结构下的性质。在神经科学和金融两个真实数据集上进行了应用，并与基于GAN的相关矩阵生成方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种可调谐、有原则的方式来构造适合图模型推断统计方法基准测试的相关矩阵，具有理论保证和较好的实际应用效果。

Abstract: This work addresses the generation of theoretical correlation matrices with prescribed sparsity patterns associated to graph structures. We propose a novel convex optimization framework in which an initial matrix is projected onto an elliptope under a positive semidefiniteness constraint. Several numerical schemes are implemented and compared. The problem falls within the broader class of matrix completion, where off-diagonal entries corresponding to absent edges are fixed to zero and diagonal entries are fixed to one. Beyond this structural constraint, the approach offers greater flexibility than existing methods by allowing control over the mean of the off-diagonal entry distribution, enabling the generation of correlation matrices that better reflect realistic data. This procedure is not designed to yield a uniform distribution over the feasible set; rather, it provides a principled and tunable way to construct correlation matrices suitable for benchmarking statistical methods for graphical model inference. Theoretical guarantees on the existence of solutions are established, both in the general setting and under the additional mean constraint. Simulation studies illustrate the properties of the generated matrices with respect to graph structure. The methodology is applied to two real-world datasets from neuroscience and finance, and a comparison with GAN-based correlation matrix generation is provided.

</details>


### [138] [CausalForge: A Formally Grounded, Self-Improving Agentic Framework for Automated Research in Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22511)
*Jiyuan Tan,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: CausalForge是一个基于Lean证明助手的因果推断自动化理论研究框架，结合了Causalean形式化库（7,035个机器验证声明）和CausalSmith自改进智能体流水线，通过机器验证证明和声明审计来确保研究可靠性，解决了LLM评审者不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化理论研究不仅受限于候选结果的生成，更受限于结果的可靠评估。现有LLM评审者经验上不可靠——可能接受伪造论文且检测率接近随机水平，因此需要更可靠的自动化理论验证方法。

Method: CausalForge包含两个核心组件：(1) Causalean——在语言模型辅助下经人类设计和审查构建的因果推断形式化Lean库（7,035个机器检查声明）；(2) CausalSmith——自改进智能体流水线，负责选题、提出结果、形式化陈述、构造证明并呈现成果，同时通过声明审计（将形式定理与非正式声明对比）补全内核验证。

Result: 通过完成的自主研究运行产生的成果对系统进行评估。源代码、形式化库和运行记录已公开。

Conclusion: 机器验证的证明仅能确认形式陈述从假设中推导得出，不能保证该陈述忠实反映预期的科学主张，因此系统将内核验证与声明审计相结合，以增强自动化理论研究的可靠性。

Abstract: Automating theoretical research is constrained not only by the generation of candidate results, but also by their reliable evaluation. A common approach is to close the research loop with a large language model (LLM) reviewer. However, such reviewers remain empirically unreliable: they may accept fabricated papers and detect them at rates close to chance (Bad Scientist, 2025). We present CausalForge, a framework for automated theoretical research in causal inference grounded in the Lean proof assistant. CausalForge combines Causalean, a foundational Lean library for causal inference containing 7,035 machine-checked declarations developed with language-model assistance under human design and review, with CausalSmith, a self-improving agentic pipeline that selects research topics, proposes results, formalizes statements, constructs proofs, and presents the resulting artifacts for human inspection. Because a machine-checked proof establishes only that a formal statement follows from its assumptions, not that the statement faithfully captures the intended scientific claim, the pipeline augments kernel verification with a statement audit that compares each formal theorem against the informal claim it is intended to express. We evaluate the system using artifacts produced by completed autonomous research runs. The source code, formal library, and run records are available at https://github.com/Jiyuan-Tan/CausalForge.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Semiparametric inference on identification sets in choice modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21790)
*Antoine Scheid,Jia Wan,Guy Aridor,Nathan Kallus,Aurelien Bibaut*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究离散选择模型中，基于有限选择集观测数据无法点识别未观测选择集的反事实选择概率的问题。将反事实概率表示为混合分布的线性泛函，通过线性规划刻画识别集端点，证明其路径可微性，给出端点估计量的渐近正态性，并提出了用于验证候选值是否属于识别集的EM类算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离散选择模型中，基于有限选择集的观测数据，未观测选择集下的反事实选择概率通常无法被点识别，需要刻画其识别集的边界并进行推断。

Method: 将反事实概率表示为混合分布的线性泛函，利用线性规划刻画识别集的上下端点，证明识别边界的路径可微性，建立插件估计量的渐近正态性，并设计EM类算法验证候选值是否属于识别集。

Result: 证明了识别边界具有路径可微性，进而得到端点估计量的渐近正态性；提出的EM类算法具有局部收敛保证。

Conclusion: 在缺乏点识别条件时，仍可通过线性规划方法刻画反事实概率的识别集并进行有效推断。

Abstract: In a discrete choice model, choice probabilities observed for a finite collection of choice sets may not identify a counterfactual choice probability under an unobserved choice set. We represent this counterfactual probability as a linear functional of a mixing distribution. Because the target is a functional of a distribution whose support is not restricted to a finite set, the parameter space is infinite-dimensional, while the data impose only finitely many moment restrictions. Therefore, observed choice probabilities need not point identify such a target. The identified set is defined as the set of target values compatible with observed choice probabilities. Rather than imposing conditions to ensure point identification, we characterize the identified set, and conduct inference on its lower and upper endpoints. We represent each endpoint as the value of a linear program over probability measures, and give conditions to obtain pathwise differentiability of the identification bounds. As a consequence, we are able to prove asymptotic normality of plug-in endpoint estimators. Finally, we provide an Expectation-Maximization-like algorithm for certifying membership of candidate values in the identified set and establish local convergence guarantees.

</details>


### [140] [Nonparametric Inference for Semigroup Blocks of Switching Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22183)
*Yuzhong Cheng*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对混合系统中 regime 条件转移概率与矩的短时无穷小结构不可直接观测的问题，研究了一类切换扩散过程的非参数估计方法。通过 Dynkin-Taylor 展开识别前两个块生成元系数，利用局部探针恢复切换强度、漂移和扩散项。在网格收缩下，公共设计差分抵消局部水平得到鞅阵列，非重叠二阶差分保留块内协方差并产生方差因子二。所得一阶和二阶估计量渐近正态，可学生化，支持短时近似和原始系数交互作用的设定检验。固定网格局部多项式理论与数值实验补充了恢复结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合系统中 regime 条件转移概率和矩是预测与决策的基本输入，但其短时无穷小结构无法直接观测，因此需要开发非参数估计方法从可观测的切换扩散过程及其 regime 数据中恢复这些结构。

Method: 利用 Dynkin-Taylor 展开识别前两个块生成元系数，通过局部探针（localized probes）恢复切换强度、漂移和扩散系数。在收缩网格下，采用公共设计差分（common-design difference）抵消局部水平得到鞅阵列，使用非重叠二阶差分（nonoverlapping second differences）保留块内协方差并产生方差因子二。

Result: 一阶和二阶估计量具有渐近正态性，可进行可行学生化（feasible studentization），支持短时近似（short-horizon approximation）及原始系数间交互作用的设定检验。

Conclusion: 所提出的非参数估计方法能有效恢复切换扩散过程的 regime 相关无穷小生成元结构，具有良好渐近性质，且固定网格局部多项式理论与数值研究验证了恢复结果的有效性。

Abstract: Regime-conditioned transition probabilities and moments are basic inputs for prediction and decision making in hybrid systems, but their short-time infinitesimal structure is not directly observable. We study nonparametric estimation of regime-indexed semigroup blocks for switching diffusions observed together with their regimes. A Dynkin--Taylor expansion identifies the first two block-generator coefficients, and localized probes recover switching intensities, drift, and diffusion. Under shrinking meshes, a common-design difference cancels the localized level and yields a martingale array; nonoverlapping second differences preserve within-block covariance and produce the variance factor two. The resulting first- and second-order estimators are asymptotically normal, admit feasible studentization, and support short-horizon approximation and specification checks for interactions among primitive coefficients. Fixed-mesh local-polynomial theory and a numerical study complement the recovery results.

</details>

### [141] [Self-Poisoning in Adaptive Out-of-Distribution Detection: A Sharp-Threshold Theory and Certified Label-Free Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21673)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了测试时自适应OOD检测器存在一个可证明的动力学规律：基于广义Pólya urn模型，内存库的杂质收敛到一个平均场均衡，其斜率作为再生数。当斜率低于1时杂质可控，高于1时检测器完全崩溃。通过测量发现所有编码器族的准入核均为仿射函数（R²≥0.996），斜率略低于1，表明该类检测器设计上处于近临界状态。认证准入门控可切断反馈回路，消除任何污染率下的相变。同时提出CDC方法标签自由地恢复漂移下的名义FPR。最后证明了"漂移与污染无标签不可区分"的不可逆定理，给出了标签自由自适应OOD检测的完整可能性/不可能性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应OOD检测器通过从无标注数据流更新内存库来适应分布变化，但这种自适应过程的动力学行为尚不明确，缺乏理论保证。本文旨在揭示其内在动力学规律，并探索在何种条件下检测器会崩溃，以及如何设计可认证安全的门控机制来避免这种崩溃。

Method: 将内存库杂质建模为广义Pólya urn，证明其几乎必然收敛到平均场均衡。通过测量不同编码器族的准入核（admission kernel）发现均为仿射函数，且斜率接近1。提出认证准入门控（certified admission gate）只读取冻结保留集以切断反馈回路，并提出CDC方法修复静态校准漂移。最后利用信息论证明两世界不可逆定理。

Result: 96个设置下预测阈值与经验崩溃点匹配，未加门控的字典损失高达0.163 AUROC。认证准入门控在所有污染率（包括对抗性设置）下消除相变，同时标签自由地控制假阳性。CDC在漂移影响的所有细胞上恢复名义FPR。不可逆定理给出了标签自由自适应OOD检测的功率上界。

Conclusion: 本文给出了标签自由自适应OOD检测的完整可能性/不可能性表征：在无标签条件下，漂移和污染不可区分，存在信息论上限；但通过认证准入门控和CDC方法，可以在理论上可证明地接近这一上限，实现安全的自适应OOD检测。

Abstract: Test-time adaptive out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors update a memory bank from the unlabelled stream. We show this adaptation obeys a provable dynamical law. Modelling bank impurity as a generalized Pólya urn, we prove almost-sure convergence to a mean-field equilibrium whose slope acts as a reproduction number. Below one, impurity stays benign. Above one, the bank is fully poisoned and the detector collapses. The measured admission kernel is affine ($R^2 \ge 0.996$) with slope just below one in every encoder family (a protocol signature), so this detector class is near-critical by design, and across 96 settings the predicted threshold matches the empirical collapse, where ungated dictionaries lose up to $0.163$ AUROC. We then prove that a certified admission gate, reading only a frozen reserve, severs the feedback loop and removes the transition at every contamination rate, even adversarially, while controlling false positives label-free. For the complementary static-calibration failure under drift we give CDC, which restores nominal FPR label-free on all tested drift-affected cells. Finally we prove a two-world impossibility theorem. Drift and contamination are indistinguishable without labels, forcing a closed-form power ceiling our procedure approaches. Together these give a complete possibility/impossibility characterization of label-free adaptive OOD detection.

</details>


### [142] [An Introduction to Bayesian and Frequentist Simulation-Based Inference with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21702)
*Maximilian Dax,Theo Heimel,Gilles Louppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于机器学习的模拟推断（SBI）在科学和工程逆问题中的应用，涵盖贝叶斯与频率统计框架、神经后验估计和神经似然估计等方法，以及其在经验贝叶斯、去折叠任务中的扩展，并讨论了验证方法和局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模拟数据生成能力的提升，传统推断方法在高维或复杂模型下计算困难。机器学习驱动的SBI方法能够高效地利用模拟器进行参数推断和探测器效应反演，解决了科学和工程中逆问题求解的计算瓶颈。

Method: 概述贝叶斯和频率统计框架；介绍基于机器学习的SBI方法，包括神经后验估计（NPE）和神经似然估计（NLE）；展示这些方法在参数估计、经验贝叶斯和去折叠（unfolding）任务中的应用；讨论验证推断结果的方法和SBI的局限性。

Result: NPE和NLE能够有效替代传统MCMC方法，在高维参数空间和复杂似然场景下实现快速、可扩展的参数估计；同样的方法框架可推广到经验贝叶斯和去折叠问题；提供了系统的验证策略。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的SBI方法为科学和工程中的逆问题提供了强大且灵活的工具，但在实际应用中需注意模型验证、仿真与真实数据匹配等关键局限。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) with machine learning is an increasingly important tool for solving inverse problems in science and engineering, including parameter inference and the inversion of detector effects.
