We provide an overview of the Bayesian and frequentist statistical frameworks, describe how machine-learning-based SBI methods, such as neural posterior estimation and neural likelihood estimation, can be used for parameter estimation within these frameworks, and show that the same methods can also be applied to Empirical Bayes or unfolding tasks. We also discuss how to validate inference results and the limitations of SBI with machine learning.

</details>


### [143] [A Defense of the Quadratic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21716)
*Alexandru Meterez,Pranav Ajit Nair,Depen Morwani,Cengiz Pehlevan,Sham Kakade,Alex Damian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在150M参数、3B tokens的LLM预训练中验证二次模型（泰勒展开至二阶）能够准确预测长达10%训练窗口的优化动力学，并通过Hessian谱分析与局部线性稳定性测试揭示了批大小、预处理器和训练时间对优化动态的影响，表明二次模型可作为预训练优化的理论可处理代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络损失景观的复杂性迫使优化理论依赖理想化模型，而这些模型在理论可处理性与描述真实优化动态准确性之间存在权衡。本文旨在压力测试最简单的优化模型——二次模型，检验其在LLM预训练场景中的预测能力。

Method: 1) 在150M参数LLM和3B tokens训练数据上，对中间检查点的模型和损失函数进行泰勒展开，构建局部二次模型；2) 使用Lanczos求积法进行深度探针估计Hessian谱的尾部结构；3) 在中间检查点测试局部线性稳定性，并与理论预测进行比较。

Result: 1) 二次模型能准确预测长达10%训练窗口内的优化动力学；2) Hessian谱在特征值和特征向量上展现出显著结构，且依赖于批大小、预处理器和训练时间；3) LLM优化通常处于随机边缘稳定性状态，其性质由批大小决定。

Conclusion: 二次模型可以作为预训练优化动态的理论可处理代理，为理解LLM优化行为提供了简化但有效的分析框架。

Abstract: Due to the complexity of neural network loss landscapes, optimization theory is forced to rely on idealized models, and there is generally a tradeoff between how theoretically tractable the model is, and how accurately it describes the true optimization dynamics. In this work, we stress test the simplest possible model of optimization -- the quadratic model -- and show that it can be surprisingly predictive in an LLM setting with 150M parameters and 3B training tokens. Specifically, we show that Taylor expanding the model and the loss function at intermediate checkpoints through training can accurately predict the optimization dynamics over windows that can last up to 10\% of training. Having established this agreement, we then turn to analyzing the structure of these local quadratic optimization problems through two lenses: the Hessian spectrum and local stability. Using Lanczos quadrature with extremely deep probes, we are able to estimate the Hessian spectrum deep into the tail, and we find a surprising amount of structure in both the eigenvalues and eigenvectors, which depends on the batch size, preconditioner, and training time. We also empirically test local linear stability at intermediate checkpoints and compare it to theoretical predictions to demonstrate that optimization in LLMs typically occurs at a stochastic edge of stability, whose nature is also determined by batch size. Our results indicate the quadratic model may be a theoretically tractable proxy for pretraining optimization dynamics.

</details>


### [144] [Scaling Laws for Classical Machine Learning on Tabular Data: A Benchmark Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21866)
*Kaihua Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在大规模课堂场景下复现了经典机器学习学习曲线研究：127名学生使用18个表格数据集和6种模型，进行了11,536次训练，拟合出1,648条幂律曲线。主要发现：(1) 幂律拟合良好，树模型表现最优；(2) 同一模型族内的指数具有近似可预测压缩性（非普遍性）；(3) 即使固定随机种子，不同实现间仍存在显著方差（平均CV(b)=0.144）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习曲线研究多在小规模（单曲线、单团队、少量数据单元）上拟合幂律，缺乏大规模、分布式、可复现的验证。本文旨在通过课堂规模的分布式复制实验，检验幂律定律在大规模多模型多数据集场景下的适用性、模型族内指数的可迁移性，以及实现差异对结果的影响。

Method: 127名学生每人按固定协议在3个分配的数据集上运行实验，覆盖18个表格分类/回归数据集和6种模型（Boosting、Random Forest、SVM、Linear/Logistic、Ridge、Lasso），共11,536次训练运行。对每个（模型，数据集）单元拟合幂律曲线 error(N)=aN^(-b)+c，共1,648条曲线。通过R²评估拟合优度，通过指数分布分析和AIC比较评估模型族内共享指数假设，通过固定随机种子下的独立重实现评估实现方差。

Result: (1) 77.7%的单元R²>0.8，树模型（Boosting占50%数据集、RF占33%）在全数据量下表现最优，线性模型在分类任务上表现不佳。(2) 5/6个模型族内，单一共享指数预测跨数据集曲线的R²差距<0.011，但AIC更偏好无约束拟合，曲线塌缩部分（32-58%点在±0.5 dex内）；Lasso完全失败，Ridge在留一数据集验证中脆弱。(3) 固定random_state=42后，独立实现之间指数b的变异系数均值CV(b)=0.144，源自协议中未约束部分（预处理、编码、缺失值处理）。

Conclusion: 幂律学习曲线在大规模多模型多数据集场景下仍近似成立，但存在重要边界：树模型统治大样本场景、模型族内指数具有近似可预测性（而非普遍性）、实现方差不可忽略。研究提供了聚合曲线、逐单元拟合结果和实用的数据需求表（达到目标误差0.15所需样本量N*），为学习曲线理论提供了大规模实证基础。

Abstract: Prior classical-ML learning-curve work fits power laws to tree, linear, and kernel models on tabular data, but at small scale: typically one curve, one team, a handful of cells. We present a distributed classroom-scale replication: 127 students each ran a fixed protocol on 3 assigned datasets, drawn from 18 tabular classification and regression datasets and 6 model families (Boosting, Random Forest, SVM, Linear/Logistic, Ridge, Lasso), yielding 11,536 training runs and 1,648 fitted power-law curves of the form error(N) = a N^(-b) + c. Three findings. (1) Power laws fit: R^2 > 0.8 on 77.7% of cells, with tree ensembles dominating at full data (Boosting 50% of datasets, RandomForest 33%; linear models underperform on classification). (2) Approximate shared exponents within a model family: for 5 of 6 families, a single family-level exponent predicts each family's cross-dataset curves nearly as well as per-dataset exponents (R^2 gap < 0.011), though AIC favors the unconstrained fit and curve collapse is partial (32-58% of points within +/-0.5 dex). We frame this as approximate predictive compressibility, not dataset-independent universality; Lasso fails outright (negative control) and Ridge is fragile under leave-one-dataset-out. (3) Replicator-implementation variance: with random_state=42 fixed, independent re-implementations of the same protocol still differ by mean CV(b) = 0.144 on the fitted exponent -- not seed variance, but the spread induced by unconstrained parts of the protocol (preprocessing, encoding, missing-value handling). We release the aggregated curves, per-cell fits, and a practical data-requirement table for N* to reach target error 0.15.

</details>


### [145] [From Score Approximation to Distribution Approximation in Score-Based Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22199)
*Lan V. Truong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了分数匹配扩散模型中神经网络分数函数逼近与生成分布逼近之间的严格定量联系，证明了如果神经网络足够准确地逼近真实分数函数，则逆向扩散模型生成的分布与目标分布之间的KL散度存在显式上界，该上界由分数逼近误差、扩散噪声调度和终端先验失配决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型在生成建模中取得了显著成功，但其近似理论基础尚不完整。经典通用逼近定理保证神经网络可以逼近分数函数，但尚不清楚这种逼近保证是否能转化为逆向扩散过程生成的概率分布的逼近保证。

Method: 结合Hornik通用逼近定理、路径空间上的Girsanov定理以及相对熵的数据处理不等式，推导出分布逼近误差关于分数逼近误差、扩散噪声调度和终端先验失配的显式上界。

Result: 建立了从分数函数逼近到生成分布逼近的定量保证：若神经网络充分准确地逼近真实分数函数，则逆向扩散模型生成的分布与目标数据分布在KL散度上接近，误差受限于分数逼近误差、噪声调度和终端先验失配三个因素。

Conclusion: 本文基于经典神经网络逼近理论发展了一种近似理论分析，提供了一个简单且显式的保证，将神经网络的分数函数逼近与逆向扩散模型生成的概率分布逼近联系起来，补充了近期在有限样本统计设置和数据分布结构假设下的分数逼近研究。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models have achieved remarkable empirical success in generative modeling, yet their approximation-theoretic foundations remain incomplete. In particular, although classical universal approximation theorems guarantee that neural networks can approximate score functions, it remains unclear whether such approximation guarantees translate into approximation of the probability distributions generated by reverse diffusion processes. In this paper, we establish a rigorous quantitative connection between these two notions. Specifically, we prove that if a neural network approximates the true score function sufficiently accurately, then the probability distribution generated by the corresponding reverse diffusion model is close to the target data distribution in Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence, up to an irreducible mismatch between the terminal distribution of the forward diffusion process and the prior used to initialize the reverse process. More precisely, we derive an explicit upper bound on the distribution approximation error in terms of the score approximation error, the diffusion noise schedule, and the terminal prior mismatch. Our analysis combines Hornik's universal approximation theorem, Girsanov's theorem on path space, and the data processing inequality for relative entropy. Complementary to recent work that studies score approximation under finite-sample statistical settings and structural assumptions on the data distribution, our work develops an approximation-theoretic analysis based on classical neural network approximation theory. The resulting theorem provides a simple and explicit guarantee linking neural network approximation of score functions to approximation of the probability distributions generated by reverse diffusion models.

</details>


### [146] [An Insight on Evaluation Metrics Under the Imbalanced Case of Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22286)
*Romain Hermary,Nesryne Mejri,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了AUROC、AUPR、F1-score和MCC四种异常检测指标在严重类别不平衡下的行为特性，通过构建"指标景观"可视化方法，揭示了不同异常比率下各指标对真阳性率和真阴性率的偏好与稳定性差异，为跨不同不平衡程度数据集的结果比较提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测中固有的严重类别不平衡问题使得常用评估指标的解释变得困难，现有研究缺乏对这些指标在不同不平衡程度下行为的系统性理解和可视化分析。

Method: 研究聚焦于四种常用异常检测指标（AUROC、AUPR、F1-score、MCC）的"指标景观"可视化分析，建立指标值与真阳性率和真阴性率之间的映射关系，从而直观展示不同不平衡比率下各指标的偏好和稳定性特征。

Result: 揭示了在不同异常比率下各指标的行为差异：AUROC偏向高真阴性率，AUPR侧重阳性类的精确率，F1-score和MCC在不同平衡水平下表现出不同的偏好和稳定性特征。

Conclusion: 为异常检测研究者和实践者提供了跨不平衡比率数据集的结果比较与解释的实用指导，强调了在不同异常率下选择恰当评估指标的重要性。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is inherently characterised by severe class imbalance, making the interpretation of evaluation metrics challenging. Although metrics such as AUROC, AUPR, F1-score, and MCC are widely used, their values convey different meanings depending on the anomaly ratio. In this work, we analyse the behaviour of those four common anomaly detection metrics under varying levels of imbalance. We focus on the study of metric landscapes, visualisations that relate metric values to true positive and true negative rates, providing an intuitive view of metric preferences and stability. Our analysis offers practical guidance for interpreting and comparing anomaly detection results across datasets with different imbalance ratios.

</details>


### [147] [Local-Global Geometric Insights for Graph Neural Networks via Entropic Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22381)
*Rachid Caich,Yassine Abbahaddou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于熵的全局曲率（Entropic Curvature）用于图神经网络，通过Wasserstein测地线上的熵位移凸性来刻画信息传播，并推导了oversmoothing和oversquashing的统一理论框架，同时设计了三种实用机制（E-Gate、ENT编码、MCR），在多个基准上取得了优于SDRF、FoSR等方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图曲率（如Ollivier-Ricci和Forman曲率）主要基于局部边级比较，无法真正刻画信息在长距离上的传播行为，因此难以从全局角度解决GNN中的oversmoothing和oversquashing问题。

Method: 将Lott-Sturm-Villani框架扩展到图上，通过沿Wasserstein测地线的熵位移凸性定义全局的Entropic Curvature；提出Weak Entropic Curvature代理作为其下界；由此推导Poincaré不等式、传输熵泛化界和扩张悖论；设计E-Gate聚合器、ENT结构编码和MCR重连机制。

Result: 在6个节点分类和图分类基准上，所提方法优于SDRF、FoSR、BORF、LCP和Graph Ricci Flow等现有方法。

Conclusion: 稀疏性、强谱扩张与正熵曲率在大图中不可共存，oversmoothing和oversquashing是同一曲率谱的两端；本文的理论和机制为GNN中的信息传播问题提供了统一的全局视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Curvature notions on graphs, particularly Ollivier-Ricci and Forman, have emerged as powerful tools for addressing fundamental issues in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) such as oversmoothing and oversquashing, but rely almost exclusively on local edge-level comparisons and therefore fail to certify how information actually propagates over long distances. We introduce Entropic Curvature, a global, transport-based curvature obtained by extending the Lott-Sturm-Villani framework to graphs through the displacement convexity of entropy along Wasserstein geodesics. We define a tractable Weak Entropic Curvature proxy that lower-bounds the global entropic curvature, and from it derive (i) a Poincare-type inequality controlling oversmoothing, (ii) a transport-entropy generalization bound, and (iii) an expansion paradox proving that sparsity, strong spectral expansion, and positive entropic curvature cannot coexist in large graphs, unifying oversmoothing and oversquashing as opposite ends of a single curvature spectrum. We translate the theory into three practical mechanisms, the E-Gate aggregator, the ENT structural encoding, and Midpoint-Completion Rewiring (MCR), and benchmark them against SDRF, FoSR, BORF, LCP, and Graph Ricci Flow on six node-classification benchmarks, and graph-classification.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Negative-Ridge Endpoints: Mixed-Sign Spectral Regularization via Negative-Shifted Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22474)
*Peng Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在过参数化线性回归中，早期停止的负偏移梯度下降能够突破稳定负脊端点的结构限制，通过混合符号滤波器在Marchenko-Pastur屏障下实现多项式级别的风险改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化线性回归中，弱谱方向隐式施加了类似岭惩罚的效果，而负脊虽能自然补偿但存在结构限制（极点必须低于最小非零经验特征值，且对小特征值的反收缩强于大特征值），需要探索突破这些限制的方法。

Method: 提出早期停止的负偏移梯度下降方法，其滤波器在潜在极点处光滑且支持混合符号；采用局部化Duhamel积分处理非收缩偏移动力学；在Gaussian spike-plus-flat模型中发现Marchenko-Pastur屏障；通过有限网格留出不等式将分离性传递给验证选择算法。

Result: 在显式条件下，早期停止路径在所有可容许端点上实现了多项式因子的风险改进；当尺度分离时，能够同时恢复所有头部尺度，超越正收缩和任何均匀缩放的无脊解。

Conclusion: 早期停止负偏移梯度下降有效突破了负脊的结构限制，在一般高有效秩尾部条件下，迹设定了隐式下限，平方谱控制暴露度，地板临界路径可同时恢复所有头部尺度。

Abstract: In overparameterized linear regression, many weak spectral directions act like a ridge penalty on the signal-bearing spectrum; negative ridge is the natural correction, pushing filters above one. The stable negative-ridge endpoint, however, is structurally limited: its pole must stay below the smallest nonzero empirical eigenvalue, and it anti-shrinks smaller eigenvalues more than larger ones. Early-stopped negative-shifted gradient descent escapes this constraint. Its filter is smooth at the would-be pole and mixed-sign-capable: above-ridgeless directions form a leading prefix, with lower directions shrunk or exposure-controlled while stopping sets the crossover. In a Gaussian spike-plus-flat model we discover a Marchenko-Pastur barrier: the shift that cancels the implicit penalty lies a bulk width above the smallest empirical eigenvalue, and the stopped path improves on every admissible endpoint by a polynomial factor in risk under explicit conditions. Our main theorem permits a general high-effective-rank tail: its trace sets the implicit floor, its squared spectrum controls exposure, and the floor-critical path recovers all head scales at once, beyond positive shrinkage and, once scales separate, every uniform rescaling of ridgeless. Handling the noncontractive shifted dynamics is the central technical challenge; localized Duhamel integrals control them. A finite-grid hold-out inequality transfers the separations to the validation-selected algorithm.

</details>


### [149] [Cloud-Native Evaluation-as-a-Service: A Microservices Architecture for Scalable AI Monitoring with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21623)
*Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EaaS，一种云原生参考架构，将AI评估方法（共形预测、校准评估、漂移检测、公平性监控等）实现为六个无状态Kubernetes微服务，实验验证了其覆盖一致性、漂移检测能力、公平性监控效果及低延迟性能，并与现有开源工具进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估工具缺乏将共形预测作为服务、微服务分解与DAG编排相结合的统一平台，亟需一种云原生、可组合、可扩展的评估架构。

Method: 提出EaaS架构，包含六个Kubernetes微服务：有限样本校正的自适应预测集共形预测、校准评估、RFF近似MMD漂移检测、Bootstrap置信区间公平性监控、DAG管道编排器及结果存储API，并在多个数据集上验证了覆盖一致性、漂移检测能力与公平性监控效果。

Result: 共形预测覆盖偏差<1.4个百分点；MMLU顶20 logprobs零插补需求，10%插补仅影响<1.5%覆盖；RFF-MMD对中度和重度漂移检测率100%，I类错误5-8.5%；UCI Adult数据集种族DP差距0.33；共形预测与校准服务p99延迟<2ms，RFF-MMD约500ms。

Conclusion: EaaS是目前首个将共形预测即服务、微服务分解与DAG编排相结合的AI评估平台，在覆盖一致性、漂移检测和延迟性能上表现优异。

Abstract: We present EaaS, a cloud-native reference architecture that operationalizes AI evaluation methods as six stateless Kubernetes microservices: conformal prediction with finite-sample-corrected Adaptive Prediction Sets, calibration assessment, drift detection via RFF-approximated Maximum Mean Discrepancy, fairness monitoring with bootstrap confidence intervals, a DAG-based pipeline orchestrator, and a result storage API. We validate four key methodological concerns. First, empirical coverage is consistent with the marginal conformal guarantee across K=50 random calibration/test splits, with mean coverage within 1.4 percentage points of the nominal target. Second, all four MMLU answer tokens appear in the top-20 logprobs with 0% imputation needed, and simulated imputation at 10% produces less than 1.5% coverage impact. Third, RFF-MMD achieves 100% detection power for mild and severe drift at the median heuristic bandwidth, with Type I error between 5-8.5%. Fourth, fairness monitoring on the UCI Adult Income dataset reveals significant demographic parity disparities by race (DP gap=0.33) with stable alerts across sequential batches. Conformal prediction and calibration services achieve sub-2ms p99 latency at batch size 100; RFF-MMD requires ~500ms suited for periodic batch monitoring. A comparison with four open-source tools suggests that, to the best of our knowledge, no current platform combines conformal-prediction-as-a-service, microservice decomposition, and DAG-based orchestration.

</details>


### [150] [On the Depth Scalability of Logic Gate Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21633)
*Taegun An,Dohun kim,Haebeom Lee,Changhee Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出输入锚定逻辑门网络（IALGN），通过让每个门结合隐层特征与直接输入锚点，解决了逻辑门网络（LGN）无法从深度增加中获益的问题。实验表明IALGN在超过100层时仍能持续提升精度，而优化稳定性和信息访问模式是深度可扩展的双重必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逻辑门网络（LGN）尽管通过布尔运算组合实现计算，但与传统布尔电路不同，它们无法可靠地从增加的深度中获益。作者识别出两个原因：深度松弛LGN中的优化崩溃，以及即使在跳跃偏置初始化和直通估计稳定训练后仍存在的拓扑诱导限制。因此，仅可训练性不足，更深层还必须接收支持有用计算的信息。

Method: 提出输入锚定逻辑门网络（IALGN），其中每个门将演化的隐层特征与直接输入锚点结合。这种拓扑保留了一条计算脊柱，同时使每一层都条件于原始输入。证明深度为D的路径可依赖多达D+1个输入比特，并建立了严格的逐路径深度层次。还提出了随机k锚定松弛以在不松弛脊柱的情况下改进锚点选择。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上，IALGN在超过100层时实现了固定宽度的深度-精度持续提升，而替代LGN拓扑饱和或退化。层探针、拓扑消融和有效深度分析表明，输入锚定产生了逐渐更具信息性的表示并保留了更长的计算路径。

Conclusion: 逻辑门网络中可扩展的深度既需要稳定的优化，也需要支持输入条件化细化的信息访问模式。输入锚定拓扑同时满足了这两个条件。

Abstract: Logic Gate Networks (LGNs) implement computation through compositions of Boolean operations, yet unlike classical Boolean circuits, existing LGNs do not reliably benefit from increased depth. We identify two distinct causes: optimization collapse in deep relaxed LGNs and a topology-induced limitation that persists even when skip-biased initialization and straight-through estimation stabilize training. Thus, trainability alone is insufficient; deeper layers must also receive information that supports useful computation.
  We introduce Input-Anchored Logic Gate Networks (IALGNs), in which each gate combines an evolving hidden feature with a direct input anchor. This topology preserves a computational spine while conditioning every layer on the original input. We show that a depth-D path can depend on up to D+1 input bits and establish a strict path-wise depth hierarchy. Random-k anchor relaxation further improves anchor selection without relaxing the spine.
  Across MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100, IALGNs achieve consistent fixed-width depth--accuracy improvements beyond 100 layers, whereas alternative LGN topologies saturate or degrade. Layer-wise probes, topology ablations, and effective-depth analysis show that input anchoring produces progressively more informative representations and preserves longer computational paths. These results demonstrate that scalable depth in LGNs requires both stable optimization and an information-access pattern that supports input-conditioned refinement.

</details>


### [151] [MotifRole-Diff: Risk-Optimal Role-Aware Corruption for Masked Molecular Graph Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21634)
*Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Elias Hossain,Ivan Garibay,Niloofar Yousef*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MotifRole-Diff提出了一种基于角色感知的掩码调度策略，根据分子图token的去噪难度和扰动影响分配不同的掩码率，在保持模型架构和序列空间不变的情况下，显著提升了分子图生成的有效性和FCD指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码离散扩散方法对所有token采用统一腐蚀调度，忽略了分子图中不同token角色在去噪难度和重构重要性上的显著差异，导致腐蚀策略与分子结构异质性不匹配。

Method: 提出角色感知的腐蚀过程MotifRole-Diff，根据经验测量的去噪难度和图级扰动影响，将固定掩码预算以风险最优方式分配到不同token角色上，保持模型架构、干净序列空间和无损分子图解码器不变。

Result: 在QM9上有效性从0.905提升至0.944，FCD从1.701降至1.609；在MOSES上有效性从0.920提升至0.938，FCD从2.125降至1.850；所有token类别的重构均得到改善。

Conclusion: 在匹配架构、训练预算和采样计算条件下，结构化信息指导的腐蚀策略比统一调度更有效，为串行化分子图扩散提供了更优的掩码策略。

Abstract: Masked discrete diffusion for molecular graph generation typically applies a uniform corruption schedule to all tokens in a lossless graph-to-sequence representation, implicitly treating structurally heterogeneous molecular components as equally difficult and equally important to reconstruct. However, different molecular graph token roles exhibit substantial variation in denoising difficulty and their influence on the decoded molecule, motivating role-specific corruption strategies. We introduce MotifRole-Diff, a role-aware corruption process that allocates masking rates according to empirically measured denoising difficulty and graph-level perturbation impact while preserving the model architecture, clean sequence space, and lossless molecular-graph decoder. We formulate schedule selection as the risk-optimal allocation of a fixed masking budget across token roles. Our theorem characterizes optimality for the modeled role-weighted residual risk, while downstream generation performance is evaluated empirically. Under matched architecture, training budget, and sampling compute, MotifRole-Diff improves validity on QM9 from 0.905 to 0.944 while reducing FCD from 1.701 to 1.609, and on MOSES improves validity from 0.920 to 0.938 while reducing FCD from 2.125 to 1.850. Role-wise diagnostics further show improved reconstruction across molecular graph token categories. Together, these matched-compute results indicate that structurally informed corruption is a more effective masking strategy than uniform schedules for serialized molecular graph diffusion.

</details>


### [152] [Toward User-Conditioned Evaluation of Personal LLM Agents under Temporal Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21635)
*Pin Qian,Su Wang,Yihang Chen,Qiaolin Yu,Xiaoyuan Wang,Zhitong Guo,Zhicheng Wang,Junxian You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证现有智能体基准测试无法评估个人智能体的持续适应性，提出评估需满足四个条件（时间干预、持久状态、跨维度效应、用户状态变化），经系统审计未发现同时满足这些条件的基准协议，并提出了最小化基准设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准将工具使用、记忆、安全等能力孤立评估，但个人智能体需要跨时间、跨组件的持续适应能力，缺乏能反映真实失败传播机制的评估协议。

Method: 形式化定义个人智能体评估的四个必要条件，通过明确纳入标准对公开基准协议进行系统审计，识别接近案例但不满足全部条件。

Result: 经审计，在限定的文献范围内未发现同时满足四个条件的现有基准协议。

Conclusion: 提出了针对用户条件化适应评估的最小化基准设计和候选报告指标，为未来个人智能体评估提供了具体的设计要求。

Abstract: Personal agents maintain memories, learned skills, tool configurations, and policy state that evolve with each user. Existing agent benchmarks often evaluate these capabilities in isolation: tool benchmarks test invocation under fixed APIs, memory benchmarks test recall or forgetting, and safety benchmarks test static policy compliance. We argue that personal-agent evaluation requires a different protocol: replaying the same temporal intervention across different persistent user-conditioned states and measuring how failures propagate across agent components. We formalize this requirement as four conditions: explicit temporal intervention, persistent state across the intervention, induced cross-dimensional effects, and variation in user-conditioned state. A focused audit of public benchmark protocols selected by explicit inclusion criteria identifies several close cases. Under our explicitly narrow operationalization, we did not find a protocol in that audited set satisfying all four conditions. This claim is scoped as a focused gap analysis with bounded literature coverage. This position paper proposes a minimal benchmark design and candidate reporting metrics for user-conditioned adaptation. The result is a concrete design requirement for future personal-agent evaluation, with metrics used as reporting tools for that requirement.

</details>


### [153] [Measuring the Dependency Gap: Diagnosing Inter-Column Fidelity in Tabular Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21636)
*Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示现有合成表格数据评估指标（如逻辑回归C2ST、成对Trend分数）对列间依赖关系不敏感，导致独立建模各列的基线方法被错误判定为与真实数据无异。作者提出一种依赖感知的诊断工具（XGB-C2ST分解），将保真度分解为边际、依赖和数值-类别交叉分量。应用于流匹配生成器（TabbyFlow/EF-VFM）发现存在真实依赖缺口，破坏依赖会彻底摧毁少数类效用。该缺口并非源于平均场生成目标的结构限制（变分流匹配目标渐近精确），而是缺乏直接的依赖监督——模型容量扩大16倍也无法弥补，后验的copula修正或依赖机制同样无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成表格数据评估指标对列间依赖关系不敏感，导致独立建模（破坏所有依赖）的基线被错误地判定为与真实数据无异。这种评估缺陷在不平衡领域（如欺诈检测、临床风险）中尤为严重，因为少数类的判别信号大量存在于列间依赖结构中。

Method: 提出依赖感知的保真度诊断方法：将基于强分类器（XGBoost）的双样本检验（C2ST）分解为边际分量、依赖分量和数值-类别交叉分量，以最坏情况的完全因子化参考（所有依赖被破坏）和最好情况的真实数据参考为锚点。应用于流匹配生成器（TabbyFlow/EF-VFM）。

Result: 1）标准指标遗漏了真实的依赖缺口；2）破坏依赖彻底摧毁少数类效用；3）生成器的残差缺口带来较小但一致的效用损失；4）缺口并非源于平均场目标的结构限制（变分流匹配目标渐近精确）；5）模型容量扩大16倍无法弥合缺口；6）后验的copula修正、依赖机制等廉价干预均无效。

Conclusion: 合成表格数据生成中的依赖缺口是顽固的，源于缺乏直接的依赖监督而非容量或结构限制。没有廉价的后验修补方案能解决该问题，对依赖修复的简单假设提出了警示。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data is valued for preserving not only each column's marginal distribution but the dependencies between columns -- structure that carries much of the discriminative signal for minority classes in imbalanced domains such as fraud and clinical risk. Yet the metrics most commonly used to certify synthetic tabular data are, we show, largely blind to inter-column dependency: a baseline that models every column independently (and therefore destroys all dependency) is judged indistinguishable from real data by the logistic-regression C2ST, and the pairwise Trend score is only partially sensitive. We introduce a dependency-aware fidelity diagnostic that decomposes a strong classifier two-sample test (XGB-C2ST) into marginal, dependency, and numerical-categorical cross components, anchored between a worst-case fully-factorized reference (all dependency destroyed) and a best-case real-data oracle. Applying it to a state-of-the-art flow-matching generator (TabbyFlow/EF-VFM), we find a real dependency gap that standard metrics miss; destroying dependency outright collapses minority-class utility, and the generator's residual gap carries a smaller, consistent utility cost. We then ask whether this gap reflects a structural limitation of mean-field generative objectives. It does not: consistent with recent recovery results for variational flow matching, the objective is asymptotically exact. Yet the gap is stubborn -- a 16x increase in model capacity does not close it -- pointing to the absence of direct dependency supervision rather than a capacity or structural limit. Consistent with this, and because the residual gap is higher-order, no cheap intervention closes it: not an in-model dependency mechanism, not post-hoc copula correction, and not the 16x capacity increase -- a caution for a field that assumes such fixes help.

</details>


### [154] [Quasi-Monte Carlo Initialization for Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21637)
*Julian G. Soltes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了准蒙特卡洛（QMC）权重初始化在元强化学习中的有效性，发现其在相似任务上优于正交初始化，但在不相似任务上正交初始化更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索准蒙特卡洛（QMC）权重初始化方法在元强化学习中的应用效果，以提升训练收敛速度和任务泛化能力。

Method: 采用准蒙特卡洛方法进行权重初始化，通过多种采样方法约束基于种群的搜索，从基线任务集合中聚合最优先验，并在现代基准环境中与正交初始化（SB3默认）进行对比。

Result: 在相似的连续控制环境中，QMC元先验相比正交初始化在训练收敛上表现出改进；但在不相似的任务中，正交初始化在无偏搜索中全局表现更优。

Conclusion: QMC权重初始化在相似任务泛化上具有优势，而正交初始化在不相似任务上更具鲁棒性，两种方法各有适用场景。

Abstract: This paper explores the efficacy of quasi-Monte Carlo (QMC) weight initialization for meta-reinforcement learning within modern benchmark environments. Various sampling methods are used to bound a population-based search and aggregate an optimal prior from a baseline set of tasks. The QMC meta-priors show improvements in training convergence compared to modern orthogonal (SB3) defaults when extrapolated to similar unseen continuous control environments. In dissimilar tasks, the orthogonal orientation was globally superior for an unbiased search.

</details>


### [155] [Toward Goal-Agnostic Joint-Embedding Predictive Control of Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21644)
*Jonathan Gallagher,Roberto Guglielmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)的无目标控制框架，用于偏微分方程。该框架使用离线训练的小型2D ViT编码器和动作条件潜动态，冻结后通过MPPI控制器复用。研究发现，将控制目标应用于显式物理可观测量（在单射条件下）优于在潜空间中最小化欧氏距离。在线性动能探针上对冻结潜卷积分步的预测达到了R²=0.989，且在2D Navier-Stokes基准测试中，基于动能探针的规划显著优于潜空间L²规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常需要为每个具体控制目标重新训练模型，缺乏通用的无目标控制框架。本文希望构建一个目标无关的潜动态模型，使其能够复用多种下游控制任务，同时探索在控制中应使用物理可观测量还是潜空间距离作为优化目标。

Method: 采用JEPA架构训练一个小型2D ViT编码器和动作条件潜动态模型；离线无监督训练后冻结模型；使用MPPI控制器进行规划控制；比较两种规划目标：潜空间L²距离与通过线性探针获得的显式物理可观测量（动能）；在2D Navier-Stokes方程上进行实验验证。

Result: 线性动能探针在冻结潜卷积分步上达到R²=0.989；在PDE Control Gym 2D Navier-Stokes基准上，KE探针规划将原生奖励从-12.08±0.86提升至-10.90±0.91，速度场RMSE从0.0765降至0.0692；在三个非周期性目标上，KE规划将晚期场RMSE降低53%（0.0220 vs 0.0469），赢得所有30个配对回合；冻结模型还支持通过直接调节KE实现稳态配置的稳定控制，平均相对误差2.7%。

Conclusion: 潜动态模型可以保持动态性和目标无关性，而校准后的物理可观测量（保证唯一延拓性质）可能是比潜空间距离更好的控制目标。但该方法对测量噪声和像素缺失较为敏感。

Abstract: We present a goal-agnostic control framework for partial differential equations (PDEs) built around a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA). The small 2D ViT encoder and action-conditioned latent dynamics are trained offline without a reward or downstream goal, frozen, and reused by a model-predictive path integral (MPPI) controller. We find that when available, the control objective is better applied to an explicit physical observable (provided injectivity) than to minimizing raw Euclidean distance ($L^2$) in the learned latent space. For a learned linear kinetic-energy (KE) probe on frozen latent rollouts we can reproduce held-out trajectories with $R^2=0.989$, while requiring no change to the underlying world model. On the PDE Control Gym 2D Navier--Stokes benchmark, using KE-probe planning improves the matched 50-episode native reward from $-12.08\pm0.86$ for latent-$L^2$ planning to $-10.90\pm0.91$ (95\% CI), while lowering last-quarter velocity-field RMSE from $0.0765$ to $0.0692$. Across three intentionally withheld, dissimilar, aperiodic targets, KE planning lowers late field RMSE by $53\%$ relative to latent-$L^2$ planning ($0.0220$ versus $0.0469$), winning all 30 paired episodes. The same frozen model also supports controls targeting stabilization around a steady configuration via direct regulation of KE achieving $2.7\%$ mean relative error. While the latent probe is brittle to measurement noise and missing pixels, we believe the results support the claim that latent dynamics can remain both dynamic and goal-agnostic while calibrated observables (granted they guarantee unique continuation) may be a better objective for state control

</details>


### [156] [Multi-Horizon Consistency as Geometry: When Latent Dynamics Contract, and When They Do Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21645)
*Kavya Bhand,Aadi Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了视频预测器中多步潜在一致性权重λ对状态转移几何结构的影响。在Moving-MNIST上，提高λ从0到0.8显著降低了潜在扩张指标L20（4.96→1.01）和预测误差E20（减半），但在Pendulum、CartPole和KTH Actions上未见同样效果。中介分析显示λ与L20强相关（r-hat=0.94），且L20与随机强迫噪声η之间存在线性关系（斜率~1.82，R²=0.96）。结论：软一致性约束可推动被动视频向近收缩带逼近，但该效应具有领域局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常用多步潜在一致性作为训练技巧，但其对状态转移几何（transition geometry）的具体影响机制尚不明确，缺乏系统性诊断分析。

Method: 将λ作为诊断控制变量，使用经验扩张代理指标L20,q95和20步预测误差E20进行测量；在Moving-MNIST、Pendulum-v1、CartPole-v1和KTH Actions等数据集上实验；采用配对t检验、Wilcoxon检验、关联中介分析（非随机化）、随机强迫噪声η的线性拟合等分析方法；并进行了架构基线、外源压力、WorldTest、MPC、缩放等防御性检查。

Result: 1) MMNIST上λ从0升至0.8使L20从4.96降至1.01（p=0.005），E20减半（0.365→0.177，p=1.1e-13），6个种子中有4个L<1；2) 该效应在Pendulum、CartPole、KTH Actions上不复现；3) 中介分析r-hat=0.94（CI [0.88,1.00]）；4) 在λ=0.8下L20与η呈线性关系（斜率1.82，R²=0.96），且在不同控制域可通过校准η_eff统一到同一曲线；5) λ=0.4和1.2时L20(η)斜率相近（~1.69和~2.00）。

Conclusion: 软一致性约束能够将被动视频预测推向近收缩的潜在状态转移带，但这种效应受到领域限制（domain-limited），在有动作条件或更复杂视频数据上不成立。L20与随机强迫噪声的线性关系为理解该效应提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Multi-horizon latent consistency is a common training knob in video predictors and world models, but practitioners rarely know what it does to transition geometry. We treat lambda, the weight on multi-step latent agreement, as a diagnostic control and measure an empirical expansion proxy L20,q95 together with horizon-20 prediction error E20. On Moving-MNIST (n=6 seeds at the critical pair), raising lambda from 0 to 0.8 cuts L20 from 4.96 +/- 2.01 to 1.01 +/- 0.06 (paired t p=0.005, Wilcoxon p=0.031) and halves E20 (0.365 to 0.177, paired t p=1.1e-13). Four of six seeds cross L<1 at lambda=0.8. The same loss does not produce population L<1 on action-conditioned Pendulum-v1 or CartPole-v1, nor on KTH Actions video, even when E20 improves. An associational mediation analysis on MMNIST gives r-hat=0.94 (95% CI [0.88, 1.00], n=27, B=2000); lambda was not randomized. Defensive checks (architectural baselines, exogenous stress, WorldTest, MPC, scaling) mostly support a narrow claim: soft consistency can push passive video toward a near-contractive band, and that band is domain-limited. A stochastic-forcing law L20 ~ 1.23 + 1.82 eta at lambda=0.8 (bootstrap slope CI [1.73, 1.92], R^2=0.96) unifies control domains on the same curve via calibrated eta_eff. Complete joint slices at lambda in {0.4, 1.2} (30/30 cells, 5 eta x 3 seeds) show comparable linear L20(eta) slopes (~1.69 and ~2.00); we do not fit a continuous (lambda, eta) surface. We do not report DreamerV3 or TD-MPC2 returns.

</details>


### [157] [Adjustment Speed as a Safety Constraint for Nonstationary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21646)
*Timothy Tomashevskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将**调整速度**作为非平稳强化学习的安全约束，通过对比环境变化所需的适应需求与系统的恢复能力，在预测到适应需求超出能力时主动收紧动作空间并启用动作层面防护，以减少不安全行为。实验表明该方法能有效减少环境变化期间的违规行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法通常假设环境平稳，未将适应速度视为安全问题。然而在非平稳环境中，延迟适应会导致瞬态不安全行为，亟需一种考虑适应可行性的安全框架。

Method: 提出适应可行性框架：利用学习到的上下文表示和短视距上下文预测来估计适应需求，并与智能体的可达适应能力对比；当预测的适应需求超过校准恢复能力时，主动收紧可行动作集并启用动作级防护。

Result: 在非平稳驾驶环境中，该方法主要减少了与环境变化对齐的短视距窗口内的安全违规；防护在峰值和尾部风险抑制上更保守，优化级调整进一步减少了短视距开关条件违规。

Conclusion: 适应可行性可作为非平稳强化学习的实用安全原则，主动干预能在环境变化期间有效改善安全性。

Abstract: Ensuring safety in reinforcement learning under nonstationarity requires determining whether a learning system can safely adapt to forecasted environmental change within the required recovery horizon. Existing safe reinforcement learning methods typically assume stationary environments and do not explicitly consider adaptation speed as a safety concern. However, when environments evolve over time, delayed adaptation may result in transient unsafe behavior.
  This paper proposes adjustment speed as a safety constraint for nonstationary reinforcement learning. The central idea is to define safety in terms of adaptation feasibility: future states or regions may become unsafe when the adaptation required to remain safe exceeds the learning system's calibrated recovery capacity. The proposed framework uses learned context representations and short-horizon context forecasts to estimate adaptation demand and compare it with the agent's achievable adaptation capacity.
  When predicted adaptation demand exceeds the calibrated recovery capacity, the framework proactively tightens the admissible action set and activates an action-level shield to reduce unsafe behavior before violations occur.
  Experiments in a nonstationary driving environment show that the proposed approach primarily reduces safety violations in short-horizon windows aligned with context changes. Ablation studies further show that shielding is more conservative for peak- and tail-risk suppression, while optimization-level adjustment provides additional reductions in short-horizon switch-conditioned violations.
  These results support adaptation feasibility as a practical safety principle for reinforcement learning under nonstationarity and demonstrate that proactive intervention can improve safety during periods of environmental change.

</details>


### [158] [A Drift Stable Quantum Federated Learning for Intelligent Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21647)
*Shanika Iroshi Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DUQFL-Prox框架，通过深度展开局部优化（自适应展开SPSA更新）和近端项约束，解决量子联邦学习中客户端漂移、数据异质性和不公平性问题。在金融欺诈检测和基因分类任务上，相比标准QFL基线，该方法在稳定性、泛化能力和客户端公平性上均有所提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子联邦学习中，异构客户端数据和噪声量子优化常导致局部更新不稳定、客户端漂移以及客户端之间的不公平性问题，现有方法难以同时保证聚合模型的准确性和客户端级别的可靠性及公平性。

Method: 提出DUQFL-Prox框架：1）每个客户端使用自适应深度展开SPSA（同时扰动随机逼近）更新代替固定局部优化器；2）引入近端项使局部模型逼近全局模型；3）轻量级控制器学习每步特定的优化参数以提升聚合后性能。

Result: 在金融欺诈检测和基因分类任务上，DUQFL-Prox相比标准QFL基线在稳定性、泛化能力和客户端公平性方面均取得更优结果。

Conclusion: 深度展开的量子联邦学习能够支持异构分布式环境中更可靠、更公平的智能服务（如隐私敏感的分布式决策系统）。

Abstract: Quantum federated learning enables distributed clients to train quantum neural networks without sharing local data, making it promising for privacy-aware intelligent services. Intelligent services in this context refer to privacy-sensitive distributed decision systems, such as fraud detection and genomic classification, where reliable and fair client-level learning is as important as the accuracy of the aggregate model. However, heterogeneous client data and noisy quantum optimization often cause unstable local updates, client drift, and unfair performance between clients. This paper proposes DUQFL-Prox, a drift-stable quantum federated learning framework based on deep-unfolded local optimization. Instead of using a fixed local optimizer, each client performs adaptive unfolded SPSA updates, while a proximal term keeps the local model close to the global model. A lightweight controller learns step-specific optimization parameters to improve post-aggregation performance. Experiments on financial fraud and genomic classification tasks show that DUQFL-Prox improves stability, generalization, and client fairness compared with standard QFL baselines. The results suggest that deep-unfolded quantum federated learning can support more reliable and fair intelligent services in heterogeneous distributed environments.

</details>


### [159] [Physiological Signals as a Forensic Modality for Talking-Face Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21776)
*Othmane Harraq,Tamer Aldwairi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对说话人脸（TF）深度伪造生成技术，提出基于rPPG生理信号的检测框架，使用RhythmFormer提取波形并训练轻量级分类器。在Celeb-DF++ TF子集上，1D ResNet达到AUC 0.806，距最佳通用检测器仅差2.4个点。验证了不同TF生成器的检测难度存在显著差异（AUC从0.985到0.690），且排名稳定，揭示了可解释的生理特性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像级检测器无法有效识别TF深度伪造视频（因为TF生成没有真实视频作为基础来继承生理特征），而rPPG生理信号检测在此任务上有独特优势。需验证生理信号作为检测模态的有效性。

Method: 通过RhythmFormer提取逐视频rPPG波形，训练一组轻量级分类器（如1D ResNet）区分真实与合成的生理信号。在严格的主体无关协议下评估（训练集与测试集身份完全分离）。

Result: 1D ResNet在Celeb-DF++ TF子集上AUC 0.806，EER 27.8%；复现DeepFakesON-Phys发现其在TF数据上从AUC 0.999退化为0.622；不同TF生成器的AUC范围0.985~0.690，排名跨协议完全稳定。

Conclusion: rPPG生理信号可作为TF深度伪造检测的有效模态；检测难度与生成器本身的生理特性相关，这构成了本文的主要理论贡献。

Abstract: Talking-face (TF) deepfake generation synthesizes photore- alistic facial video from a static source image and an au- dio signal, producing forgeries that current image-based detectors consistently fail to identify. Unlike face-swap ma- nipulation, TF synthesis has no underlying real video from which to inherit physiological characteristics, making re- mote photoplethysmography (rPPG) a uniquely motivated detection modality for this forgery category. We propose a detection framework that extracts per-video rPPG wave- forms via RhythmFormer and trains a suite of lightweight classifiers to distinguish real from synthesized physiologi- cal signals. Evaluated on the TF subset of Celeb-DF++ un- der a strict subject-independent protocol, where test identi- ties are completely separated from training identities, our 1D ResNet achieves an AUC of 0.806 and EER of 27.8%, placing it within 2.4 points of the best published general- purpose detector (Effort, ICML 2025) while operating ex- clusively on the physiological channel. We document a con- trolled reproduction study of DeepFakesON-Phys, the rep- resentative prior rPPG detector, demonstrating degrada- tion from AUC 0.999 on legacy face-swap data to 0.622 on the TF subset of Celeb-DF++. We further show that detec- tion difficulty is strongly method-dependent: AUC ranges from 0.985 (Real3DPortrait) to 0.690 (IP-LAP) across the seven TF generators, with the ranking remaining perfectly stable across all evaluation protocols. This spread reflects an interpretable physiological property of each generator rather than evaluation noise, and constitutes the primary theoretical contribution of the work.

</details>


### [160] [Shallower ReLU Network Representations via Exact Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21651)
*Kilian Rueß,Gennadiy Averkov,Florestan Brunck,Moritz Grillo,Christoph Hertrich,Georg Loho,Jack Stade,Moritz Stargalla,Matthew Sun,Martin Winter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于 n ≤ 10，n 个实数的最大值可以通过两个隐藏层的 ReLU 网络精确表示；对于 n > 10，则只需 ⌈log₅(n/2)⌉+1 个隐藏层。该结果通过将问题归约为有理线性代数并计算求解得到，改进了已有工作中 max₅ 的两层表示和 ⌈log₃(n-2)⌉+1 的层数上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 ReLU 网络在精确表示最大值函数时所需的最小隐藏层数，这是理解神经网络表达能力的基础性问题。

Method: 将最大值表示问题归约为有理数上的精确线性代数：通过对称性约简后，将必要的抵消操作编码为有限线性系统并在 ℚ 上求解验证。对于 n > 10，利用 max₁₀ 中仅由成对最大值构成的结构化第一隐藏层进行递归代入。

Result: n ≤ 10 时最大值可被两层 ReLU 网络精确表示；n > 10 时所需隐藏层数为 ⌈log₅(n/2)⌉+1 < log₅(n) + 1.5694。对于 ℝ^d 上的连续分段线性函数，当 d ≤ 9 时存在两层 ReLU 表示。

Conclusion: 改进了 Bakaev 等人 (STOC'26) 的结论，将两层表示的范围从 max₅ 扩展到 max₁₀，并将层数上界从 ⌈log₃(n-2)⌉+1 降低到 ⌈log₅(n/2)⌉+1。

Abstract: We prove that the maximum of $n$ real numbers is exactly representable by a ReLU network with two hidden layers for every $n\le 10$. The constructions are obtained by reducing the problem to exact rational linear algebra: after a symmetry reduction, the necessary cancellations are encoded in finite linear systems over $\mathbb{Q}$, which we solve and verify computationally. The representation of $\max_{10}$ has a structured first hidden layer consisting only of pairwise maxima, a feature that allows it to be recursively substituted into larger networks. We use this to show that for every $n>10$, the maximum $\max_{n}$ can be exactly represented with $\lceil{\log_5 (n / 2)\rceil}+1 < \log_5(n) +1.5694$ hidden layers. Via the generalized hinging-hyperplane representation [Wang, Sun, IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory 2005], the same depth bound holds for all continuous piecewise-linear functions on $\mathbb{R}^d$, with $d+1$ in place of $n$. In particular, every continuous piecewise-linear function on $\mathbb{R}^d$ for $d\le 9$ admits a two-hidden-layer ReLU representation. Our results improve on [Bakaev, Brunck, Hertrich, Stade, Yehudayoff, STOC'26]. In that work, the authors established a two-hidden-layer representation for $\max_{5}$ and an upper bound of $\lceil{\log_3 (n-2)\rceil}+1$ hidden layers for $\max_{n}$.

</details>


### [161] [Molt: A Scalable PyTorch-Native Training Framework for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21653)
*Jian Hu,Huiying Li,Hao Zhang,Binfeng Xu,Yifan Zhang,Shaokun Zhang,Hemil Desai,Michael Demoret,Pavlo Molchanov,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Molt是一个轻量级、PyTorch原生、开源且高性能的强化学习训练框架，旨在降低算法迭代时的工程摩擦，支持异步多模态和混合专家策略训练，统计性能堪比基于Megatron的SOTA方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习框架中，每次算法修改（新估计器、流水线阶段、 rollout 方案等）都需层层改动训练器、分布式后端和 rollout 胶水代码，迭代成本高；亟需一个简洁、可端到端追踪和修改的框架来解放研究者。

Method: 采用PyTorch原生实现，将智能体设计为普通程序，使用单异步循环训练多模态和混合专家策略，确保训练时绝不使用未由自身生成的token，保持token、策略版本和模型语义的一致性；框架代码紧凑精简，足以让研究者完全理解且可被AI编码助手整体阅读和推理。

Result: 在完全匹配的异步协议下，Molt的统计性能与基于Megatron的SOTA基线相当，证明了轻量化不牺牲性能。

Conclusion: Molt验证了通过极简设计降低强化学习研究工程成本是可行且有效的，为研究者提供了高效灵活的新选择。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning research is constant algorithm modification, new estimators, new pipeline stages, new rollout schemes, and in mainstream frameworks each change threads through layers of trainer, distributed backend, and rollout glue: the cost lands on the researcher at every iteration. Molt is a PyTorch-native training framework built to keep that cost small: a codebase compact and clean enough for a researcher to hold in their head, and for an AI coding assistant to read and reason about in its entirety, so the algorithm flow can be traced and changed end to end. The agent is an ordinary program, and one asynchronous loop trains multimodal and mixture-of-experts policies while never training on a token it did not generate, consistent in tokens, policy versions, and model semantics. Leanness does not cost performance: under a matched, fully asynchronous protocol, Molt is statistically comparable to a state-of-the-art Megatron-based stack. Molt is open source and provides recipes and containers at https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/labs-molt.

</details>


### [162] [Physically Constrained Federated Additive Models for O-RAN SLA-Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21665)
*Aubida A. Al-Hameed,Mohammed M. H. Qazzaz,Maryam Hafeez,Syed A. Zaidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Monotone FedNAM，一种面向O-RAN多租户场景的联邦可加模型，通过单调样条约束确保KPIs的物理一致性，在保护切片商业隐私的同时实现可审计的SLA违规预测，消除了所有单调性违反，将约束形状一致性从0.71提升至1.00，并减少65%上行流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN中 proactive 服务保障需要预测每个切片的SLA违规，但预测模型必须可审计且不能汇集跨基站的每切片KPI（因涉及租户商业敏感信息）。现有神经可加模型（NAMs）虽提供可审计性，但无约束的NAMs学到违反无线物理规律的效果（如信道质量变好却预测更高风险），需解决物理有效性与联邦训练下的约束保持问题。

Method: 提出Monotone FedNAM：对具有明确物理方向的KPIs采用单调样条表示，其约束通过FedAvg聚合后仍能保持；对有争议的KPIs保持无约束。模型作为Non-RT RIC rApp训练和运行，并可紧凑部署为Near-RT RIC xApp。

Result: 在ColO-RAN测试台数据集上，Monotone FedNAM消除了所有单调性违反，约束形状一致性从0.71提升至1.00，能泛化到未见过的调度策略，减少65%上行流量，代价是AUC下降0.04-0.07。

Conclusion: 物理约束的联邦可加模型能够支持多租户O-RAN服务保障中可审计的SLA风险推理，在保护商业隐私的同时确保物理合理性。

Abstract: Proactive service assurance in O-RAN requires predicting per-slice SLA violations before they occur. The prediction model must be auditable by operators and must train across base stations without pooling per-slice KPIs, which are commercially sensitive because slices are leased to individual tenants. Neural additive models (NAMs) offer auditability because each KPI contributes through a visible shape function. However, visibility alone does not guarantee physical validity. On the ColO-RAN testbed dataset, unconstrained NAMs learn effects that contradict wireless physics, for example predicting higher risk when channel quality improves. This failure appears under both local and centralized training, and non-IID federated averaging worsens it. We present Monotone FedNAM, a federated additive model in which KPIs with unambiguous physical direction are represented as monotone splines whose constraints survive FedAvg aggregation by construction, while contestable KPIs remain unconstrained. The model trains and operates as a Non-RT RIC rApp and is compact enough for deployment as a Near-RT RIC xApp. Monotone FedNAM eliminates all monotonicity violations, raises constrained shape consistency from 0.71 to 1.00, generalizes to an unseen scheduling policy, and reduces uplink traffic by 65%, at a cost of 0.04 to 0.07 AUC. These results show that physically constrained federated additive models can support auditable SLA risk inference for multi-tenant O-RAN service assurance

</details>


### [163] [Neural Feature Governance: Extending Atom Prevalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21671)
*Idris Karel Seunda Ekwe,Patrick Tenga Shako,Ernest Parfait Fokoué*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出神经原子盛行率（NAP），一个贝叶斯框架，通过四阶段流程（BLT识别、SS-IG软变分训练、PB最优层大小选择、贝叶斯微调）实现前馈神经网络的结构化节点级模型选择，在MNIST上将激活节点压缩至8%，同时保持高精度和校准良好的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习中的亿级参数架构虽然准确率高，但缺乏透明度、计算效率和可靠的量化不确定性，神经网络压缩与可解释性仍是开放挑战。

Method: 提出NAP框架，包含四阶段流程：（1）通过迭代幅度剪枝（IMP）识别贝叶斯彩票（BLT）；（2）Spike and Slab独立高斯（SS-IG）模型的软变分训练；（3）基于Poisson-Binomial（PB）分布的最优层大小选择；（4）贝叶斯微调，最终产生稀疏、稳定、可解释且准确的模型。

Result: 在MNIST上激活节点压缩至原始密集架构的8%；回归可靠性图显示近名义预测区间覆盖（观察值93.4% vs 目标95%）；偶然-认知不确定性分解显示模型无知仅占总预测方差的3-4%。

Conclusion: NAP是一个可靠、理论扎实且计算可行的解决方案，同时实现了贝叶斯神经网络中的稀疏性、准确性、可解释性和不确定性量化。

Abstract: Neural network compression and interpretability remain open challenges in modern deep learn- ing, where billion-parameter architectures deliver impressive accuracy at the cost of trans- parency, computational efficiency, and reliable uncertainty quantification. This paper introduces Neural Atom Prevalence (NAP), a principled Bayesian framework for structured node-level model selection in feedforward neural networks. NAP introduces the neural atom (activation unit) and functions as a hybrid method operating through a four-phase pipeline: Bayesian Lottery Ticket (BLT) identification via Iterative Magnitude Pruning (IMP), soft variational training of the Spike and Slab Independent Gaussian (SS-IG) model, Poisson-Binomial (PB) optimal layer-size selection, and Bayesian fine-tuning to produce a sparse, stable, interpretable, and accurate model. Extensive empirical validation across simulated nonlinear regression, two UCI benchmark datasets (Concrete, YearPredictionMSD), and the MNIST image classification task demonstrates that NAP achieves state-of-the-art structural sparsity, reducing active nodes to as few as 8% of the original dense architecture on MNIST, while well-calibrated probabilisti- cally: the aleatoric-epistemic uncertainty decomposition reveals that model ignorance accounts for only 3 to 4% of total predictive variance across all experiments, and regression reliability diagrams confirm a near-nominal predictive interval coverage (93.4% observed against a 95% target). These results establish NAP as a reliable, theoretically grounded, and computation- ally tractable solution to the simultaneous pursuit of sparsity, accuracy, interpretability, and uncertainty quantification in Bayesian neural networks.

</details>


### [164] [Encoding Invisible Causation for Bridge Diagnostic Agents: Triple-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning with QLoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21680)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Damage Cause Encoder，通过知识三元组提取、检索增强上下文和微调比较三步，实现桥梁损伤原因自动分类。QLoRA在消费级硬件上达到87.07%准确率，相比LoRA减少72%显存占用且推理加速11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁基础设施损伤的根因（盐侵蚀、冻融、疲劳开裂等）肉眼不可见，专家诊断依赖长期积累的隐性知识。现有方法缺乏自动化潜在因果推理的能力，亟需一种能够将隐性领域知识显式编码并用于自动化诊断的方法。

Method: 三组件链式方法：(i)知识三元组提取——利用大语言模型从15-35份诊断PDF手册中提取(损伤→原因)因果三元组，存入FAISS向量库；(ii)检索增强上下文——在训练和推理时检索相关因果三元组与损伤描述拼接，将隐式知识转为显式编码器输入；(iii)系统微调比较——在固定Golden Testset上严格对比LoRA、QLoRA和QA-LoRA，确定最优参数效率方案。

Result: QLoRA在116样本分层测试集上达到87.07%准确率（与LoRA相同），推理速度提升11%，GPU显存降低72%；在100样本全10类多样化评估中超出LoRA 13个百分点。

Conclusion: QLoRA在消费级硬件上实现了内存高效、高精度的桥检诊断代理，验证了检索增强与参数高效微调相结合在工业边缘部署场景中的有效性；同时贡献了可复用的分层、去重、标注难度的Golden Testset基准。

Abstract: Bridge infrastructure deteriorates gradually, yet its root causes---salt intrusion, freezing, fatigue cracking, and others---remain invisible to the naked eye. Expert diagnosis relies on tacit knowledge built over years of practice. We address the challenge of automating this latent causal reasoning by proposing a Damage Cause Encoder that classifies 10-class damage causes from visible damage descriptions $S_i$ for use in autonomous bridge diagnostic agents. Our approach chains three components: (i)Knowledge Triple Extraction---a large language model extracts causal triples of the form (damage $\xrightarrow{\mathtt{caused\_by}}$ cause) from 15--35 diagnostic PDF manuals and indexes them in a FAISS vector store; (ii)Retrieval-Augmented Context---at training and inference time, relevant causal triples $\mathcal{C}_i$ are retrieved and concatenated with $S_i$, converting implicit domain knowledge into explicit Encoder context; (iii)Systematic Fine-tuning Comparison---we conduct a rigorous comparison of LoRA, QLoRA, and QA-LoRA on a fixed Golden Testset (116 stratified samples), demonstrating that QLoRA achieves the optimal trade-off: identical test accuracy (87.07%) to full-precision LoRA, 11% faster inference, 72% lower GPU memory, and superior generalization across diverse unseen inputs. A controlled Golden Testset---stratified, deduplicated, and difficulty-tagged---is introduced as a reusable benchmark contribution. QLoRA further outperforms LoRA by 13 percentage points on a 100-sample diverse evaluation spanning all 10 damage cause classes.These findings enable memory-efficient, high-accuracy diagnostic agents on consumer-grade hardware for edge deployment.

</details>


### [165] [CARNet Cycle-Conditioned Core Aggregation and Redistribution for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21681)
*Awsaf Tausif Adib,Md. Shahria Sarker Shuvo,Md. Estehaar Ahmed Emon,Mustafa Kamal,Fuad Rahman,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARNet是一个周期条件核心聚合与重新分配框架，通过多头核心聚合将全局循环周期信息融入高效的核心交互建模中，在线性复杂度下实现跨变量依赖建模，在多个多变量时间序列预测基准上优于Transformer和非注意力基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的方法计算复杂度为二次方，随变量数量增加扩展性差；而现有的无注意力聚合模型虽具线性复杂度，但未能显式利用数据中的全局周期性结构。

Method: 提出CARNet（周期条件核心聚合与重新分配框架），通过多头核心聚合机制将全局循环周期信息融入高效的核心交互建模中，实现线性复杂度的跨变量依赖建模。

Result: 在多个真实世界多变量预测基准上，CARNet在不同预测范围下持续优于强Transformer和非注意力基线方法。

Conclusion: CARNet通过融入全局周期信息实现了线性复杂度的跨变量依赖建模，在保持效率的同时显著提升了多变量时间序列预测性能。

Abstract: Accurately modeling cross-variate dependencies remains a key challenge in multivariate time series forecasting, particularly in the presence of strong periodic patterns. Many existing approaches rely on attention-based mechanisms that incur quadratic complexity and scale poorly with increasing numbers of variates. Recent attention-free aggregation models address this issue through linear-complexity core-based interactions, but they do not explicitly leverage the global periodic structure present in the data. To overcome this limitation, we propose CARNet, a Cycle-Conditioned Core Aggregation and Redistribution framework that integrates global recurrent cycle information into efficient core based interaction modeling via Multihead Core Aggregation. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world multivariate forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that CARNet consistently outperforms strong transformer and non-attention baselines across diverse prediction horizons while preserving linear-complexity modeling of cross-variate dependencies.

</details>


### [166] [Learning What Matters: Supervising Sparse Attention Routing with Causal Evidence Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21692)
*Jim Allchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现，注意力机制并不等同于因果依赖：模型会关注与最终答案无关的内容（如过时事实），而因果证据（通过掩码实验获得）比注意力模式更适合作为稀疏注意力选择器的训练目标。在检索任务中，基于因果依赖训练的选择器可达99%准确率，而基于注意力训练的仅41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证一个常见假设：稀疏注意力选择器通过蒸馏密集教师模型的注意力模式来训练，是否真的能捕捉到教师模型实际使用的上下文信息？

Method: 通过掩码实验，遮挡输入的不同部分并观察答案是否改变，从而区分"注意力"与"因果依赖"。对比基于注意力蒸馏与基于因果证据训练的选择器性能。

Result: 注意力和因果依赖经常不一致；蒸馏选择器继承了这种不匹配。在两步引用任务中，基于注意力训练的选择器仅41%准确率，基于因果证据的达99%。Qwen2.5-3B在58%的矛盾事实样本中更关注过时事实却回答正确。

Conclusion: 注意力展示的是模型"看"的位置，而非答案实际"依赖"的内容；因果依赖作为训练目标始终匹配或优于注意力。

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces the cost of long contexts by allowing each query to read only selected parts of the input. These selectors are often trained by distilling the attention patterns of a dense teacher, assuming that attention reveals which context the teacher actually uses. We test that assumption on retrieval tasks where the evidence for each answer is known exactly. By masking parts of the context and measuring whether the answer changes, we find that attention and causal dependence often disagree, and distilled selectors inherit the mismatch. Teachers attend to outdated facts they have learned to ignore, and their attention can vary across training runs even when they rely on the same evidence. In a two-step reference task, attention at the answer skips the intermediate step because it was resolved earlier in the forward pass: a selector trained on attention achieves 41% accuracy, while the same selector trained on causal evidence reaches 99% and matches the teacher. These evidence sets require no annotation: recovered from a frozen teacher by masking alone, they train selectors to the same accuracy. We find the same conflict in pretrained models: Qwen2.5-3B gives more attention to an outdated fact than the current one on 58% of conflicting-fact examples despite answering correctly, while Gemma-2-9B rises from 56% to 99% accuracy when restricted to the two relevant sentences. Attention shows where a model looks, not necessarily what its answer depends on; across the regimes we tested, that dependence matched or outperformed attention as a training target.

</details>


### [167] [RED-PIM: Reducing Data Movement for Transformers using Processing-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21731)
*Zahra Yousefijamarani,Alaa Alameldeen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RED-PIM是一种算法-架构协同设计方法，通过将跨存储体数据传输从O(N²)降至O(N)，并将中间注意力矩阵从N×N缩小到d×d，显著降低了Transformer推理中的注意力延迟。相比基线PIM实现，推理时间减少16.05%到99.99%（几何平均66.42%），在长序列任务中表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型广泛应用于多个领域，但注意力操作中处理单元与内存之间的高数据移动量严重限制了其效率。现有基于PIM的Transformer实现存在昂贵的跨存储体通信问题，且因存储体容量有限难以扩展，注意力相关数据被迫跨存储体分割，削弱了PIM的潜在优势。

Method: 提出RED-PIM算法-架构协同设计，通过重新组织矩阵运算、局部执行计算并采用优化的数据传输策略，将跨存储体数据移动从O(N²)降至O(N)，同时将中间注意力矩阵从N×N缩小到d×d，从而降低计算开销和互连流量。

Result: 相比基线PIM实现，推理时间减少16.05%到99.99%（几何平均66.42%），长序列收益最大。在真实数据集上，长文档性能提升99.60%，短文档提升13.44%，同时保持或提升了精度。

Conclusion: RED-PIM证明了其在可扩展且高效的Transformer推理中的有效性，特别是在长序列场景下显著优于现有PIM方案。

Abstract: Transformers are widely used across many domains, including natural language processing, computer vision, web search, and DNA sequence analysis. Given their broad applicability, improving the performance of transformer models is critical. However, the high volume of data movement between processing units and memory during attention operations significantly limits their efficiency. Processing-In-Memory (PIM) mitigates this issue by performing computations directly inside memory. While prior work has proposed PIM-based transformer implementations, they suffer from costly inter-bank communication, and struggle to scale due to the limited capacity of memory banks. As a result, attention-related data must be split across banks, diminishing the potential benefits of PIM.
  In this work, we propose RED-PIM, an algorithm-architecture co-design that reduces attention latency by minimizing inter-bank data movement from O(N^2) to O(N) and shrinking intermediate attention matrices from N x N to d x d. By reorganizing matrix operations, performing computations locally, and employing an optimized data transfer strategy, RED-PIM significantly reduces computation cost and interconnect traffic. Compared to baseline PIM implementation, RED-PIM achieves inference time reductions ranging from 16.05% to 99.99% (geometric mean of 66.42%), with the largest gains on longer sequences. On real-world datasets, RED-PIM improves performance by 99.60% for long documents and 13.44% for shorter ones, while maintaining or improving accuracy. These results demonstrate RED-PIM's effectiveness for scalable and efficient transformer inference.

</details>


### [168] [Parameter-free Adaptive Sparse Attention via Compression-Based Content Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21752)
*Debarshi Kundu,Swaroop Ghosh,Vasant Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需额外参数的稀疏注意力方法，利用gzip压缩比作为动态掩码信号，在PG-19语言建模任务上以1.71 BPB超越稠密注意力(2.89)、BigBird(2.34)等基线方法，且收敛速度提升3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应稀疏注意力方法（如SBM-Transformer、Dynamic Mask Attention、NSA）虽优于固定模式掩码，但都需要额外可学习参数、自定义梯度估计器或专用CUDA内核，增加了模型复杂度和部署成本。作者希望找到一种无需任何额外参数即可生成动态稀疏掩码的方法。

Method: 计算每个块的gzip压缩比作为掩码信号：压缩比高的块（即gzip无法有效压缩的块）包含局部重复无法预测的信息，被识别为长距离注意力目标。压缩谱是输入依赖的，因此生成的稀疏掩码无需学习参数、辅助损失或自定义内核即可动态适应内容。

Result: 在PG-19字节级语言建模（92M参数，8K上下文）上达到1.71 BPB，优于稠密注意力(2.89)、BigBird(2.34)、Longformer(3.21)和SBM-Transformer(3.38)，优势在更长序列上更明显（4K到8K上下文中与BigBird差距从0.05扩大到0.63 BPB），收敛速度提升3.3倍。

Conclusion: 经典数据压缩（gzip）可作为有效的注意力掩码信号，无需额外参数即可实现比稠密注意力和现有稀疏方法更优的长序列建模性能，且序列越长优势越显著。

Abstract: Data-adaptive sparse attention masks substantially outperform fixed patterns (e.g., BigBird and Longformer) and can even exceed dense attention on long sequences. Existing adaptive approaches---including SBM-Transformer, Dynamic Mask Attention, and NSA---typically require additional learnable parameters, custom gradient estimators, or specialized CUDA kernels.
  We show that classical data compression provides an effective masking signal with \textbf{no additional parameters}. By computing per-block gzip compression ratios, we identify non-redundant content blocks and route long-range attention selectively through them. Intuitively, blocks that gzip cannot compress contain information not predictable from local repetition, making them natural long-range attention targets. Because the compression profile is input-dependent, the resulting sparse mask adapts dynamically to content without learned parameters, auxiliary losses, or custom kernels.
  On PG-19 byte-level language modeling at 92M parameters with 8K context, our method achieves 1.71 bits-per-byte (BPB), outperforming dense attention (2.89), BigBird (2.34), Longformer (3.21), and a reimplemented SBM-Transformer (3.38)---the only learned-mask baseline---by up to 1.67 BPB while adding no parameters. The advantage grows with sequence length, with the gap over BigBird widening from 0.05 BPB at 4K context to 0.63 BPB at 8K, while convergence is 3.3$\times$ faster.

</details>


### [169] [Smart predict-then-robustly-optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21773)
*Aakil Caunhye,Xuefei Lu,Belen Martin-Barragan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种鲁棒的"预测-然后-鲁棒优化"框架，通过集成鲁棒优化原理到预测-决策流水线中，应对协变量特征空间中的扰动，并建立了计算可处理的凸替代损失函数；理论上证明了该替代损失的近似误差概率呈亚高斯指数衰减，且在高概率下具有Fisher一致性；数值实验表明该方法在样本外性能和训练稳定性上均优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统"预测-然后-优化"方法假设决策时的协变量特征完美可见，但实际数据常受噪声或扰动影响，导致决策策略脆弱。为弥合这一差距，本文旨在将鲁棒优化原理直接集成到预测-决策流水线中，以抵御最坏情况下的特征扰动。

Method: 提出"smart predict-then-robustly optimize"损失函数，将鲁棒优化集成到预测-决策流水线中；建立计算可处理的凸替代损失函数来抵御最坏情况特征扰动；从理论上证明替代损失的近似误差概率依亚高斯浓度分布呈指数衰减，并证明其在高概率下具有Fisher一致性；推导该方法优于传统方法的必要条件。

Result: 理论结果表明替代损失的近似误差概率呈亚高斯指数衰减，高概率下具有Fisher一致性；数值实验验证了该方法在样本外性能和训练稳定性上持续优于传统"预测-然后-优化"方法，即使传统方法配备正则化上游预测时仍保持优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的鲁棒预测-优化框架能有效应对特征扰动，在理论和实践中均表现出优越性，为集成学习与优化领域提供了一种对数据扰动具有理论保证的鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and study a robust variant of the smart predict-then-optimize approach that accounts for prediction shifts due to disturbance in the covariate feature space. While traditional integrated-learning-and-optimization models assume that side information is perfectly revealed, empirical data-driven features are frequently corrupted or noisy at the time of decision-making, leading to fragile operational policies. To bridge this gap, we integrate principles of robust optimization directly into the predictive-prescriptive pipeline via a smart predict-then-robustly optimize loss and establish a computationally tractable convex surrogate, designed to hedge against worst-case feature perturbations. On the theoretical front, we formalize the structural validity of this surrogate by proving its approximation error probability decays exponentially according to a sub-Gaussian concentration profile. Furthermore, we establish that under mild assumptions, the surrogate is Fisher consistent with high probability. We also prove necessary conditions under which our framework outperforms standard smart predict-then-optimize and maintain its superiority even when the standard method is equipped with regularized upstream predictions. Numerical experiments validate that our robust framework consistently yields significant performance improvements over standard methods, both in out-of-sample terms and in training stability.

</details>


### [170] [Bounding the Causal Impact of ML-assisted Decision-Making via Counterfactual Correctness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21806)
*Jonathan Zhang,Erik Skalnes,Jacob Chen,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种部分识别方法，利用现有随机对照试验数据，通过单调性假设（个体层面反事实正确性和子群预测性能与结果的关系）来界定更新后机器学习模型对下游结果的因果效应，模拟研究显示该方法能比以往工作提供更紧的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML模型在高风险领域广泛应用，但模型更新后重复进行RCT评估因果效应成本过高、不可行。需要一种方法利用已有的RCT数据来评估更新模型的因果影响。

Method: 提出部分识别方法，利用两个单调性假设：（1）个体层面"反事实正确性"——正确预测不会导致更差的结果；（2）子群预测性能与结果的关系——可解释为对模型输出信任度的假设。通过这些假设从先前RCT数据中构建新模型因果效应的边界。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该方法相比以往工作能得到更紧（更信息量大）的因果效应边界。

Conclusion: 利用细粒度预测准确性与下游结果之间的关系假设，可以在不重新运行RCT的情况下，对更新模型的因果效应进行有意义的推断。

Abstract: Predictive machine learning (ML) models are increasingly used to aid human decision-makers across various high-risk domains such as healthcare and criminal justice. There is a growing recognition of the need to evaluate the causal impact of deploying these systems on downstream outcomes, such as patient survival or crime recidivism. Randomized control trials (RCTs) can provide high-quality evidence on the impact of a deployed model, but they run into a challenge: it is often infeasible to run repeated trials when models are updated or retrained to improve predictive performance. In this work, we present a partial-identification approach to using prior RCT data to construct bounds on the causal effect of a new model. The core innovation in our approach is to leverage assumptions relating fine-grained predictive accuracy to downstream outcomes. We do so via two monotonicity assumptions: first, on individual-level `counterfactual correctness' (all else being equal, a correct prediction leads to non-inferior outcomes); and second, on the relation between subgroup predictive performance and outcomes, interpretable as an assumption regarding trust in model outputs. We demonstrate our method with a simulation study, illustrating how incorporating this information can lead to more informative bounds compared to prior work.

</details>


### [171] [Data eccentricity, asymptotics of Gaussian RBF reproducing kernel Hilbert space, and kernel PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21823)
*Sergio A. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明高斯RBF核在带宽趋于无穷时，其再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）在等距缩放下渐近地等同于欧氏空间。基于此，核PCA的特征值、特征投影和主成分均收敛到经典线性PCA的结果，收敛残差为O((ρ/σ)^2)，其中ρ是数据集最大与中位成对距离之比。实验表明ρ能可靠预测数据集特定的收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高斯RBF核在大带宽极限下的渐近行为，揭示其与线性核之间的几何关系，并为基于核的方法在极限情况下趋近线性方法提供理论依据。

Method: 理论分析证明高斯RBF RKHS在带宽σ→∞时渐近等距于欧氏空间；推导核PCA与线性PCA之间的误差界为O((ρ/σ)^2)，其中ρ为数据集的几何离心率（最大与中位成对距离之比）；通过多样化数据集实验验证ρ对收敛行为的预测能力。

Result: 高斯RBF核与线性核在RKHS特征嵌入和正交PCA特征框架上差异渐近地表现为一个几何相似变换，残差为O((ρ/σ)^2)。实验证明ρ是数据集特定收敛行为的简单且可靠的预测指标。

Conclusion: 高斯RBF核在大带宽极限下行为与线性核一致，且数据集的几何离心率ρ可以有效预测核PCA向线性PCA收敛的速度。

Abstract: We show that, up to isotropic scaling, the Gaussian RBF reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) is asymptotically isometric to Euclidean space in the large bandwidth limit. This strongly suggests that kernel-based constructions reliant on metric properties of the RKHS will yield results for Gaussian RBF kernels that similarly approach those of linear kernels for large bandwidths. The asymptotic behavior of Gaussian CKA can be understood in this light. We further consider kernel PCA, showing that Gaussian RBF eigenvalues, eigenprojections, and principal components all converge to those of classical (linear) PCA as bandwidth $σ\rightarrow \infty$. For a given data representation, both the RKHS feature embeddings and the orthogonal PCA eigenframes of the two kernel types differ asymptotically by a geometric similarity transformation, up to a residual of size $O \left (\fracρσ \right )^2$, where $ρ$ is a measure of geometric eccentricity of the representation, equal to the ratio of maximum to median pairwise distance between data examples. Experiments over a diverse collection of data sets demonstrate that $ρ$ provides a simple and reliable predictor of dataset-specific convergence behavior in the top principal directions.

</details>


### [172] [A Graph-Based Control Interface for Traffic Signals on Heterogeneous Road Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21831)
*Bertil Braun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于共享图神经网络的交通信号控制接口，通过确定性关联矩阵将图神经网络的分数转换为可变大小的合法信号相位集合，实现了图规模和 junction 特定动作数量与学习参数形状的解耦。PPO实验在未见过的合成网格几何、信号覆盖变化及五个异构城市图上评估了该接口，结果表明在合成网格族内性能得以保持，信号覆盖变化暴露了分布偏移敏感性，单一城市策略在五个城市图上产生了异构结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制方法中，图神经网络的结构（如图大小、junction 特定动作数量）与学习参数形状紧密耦合，导致模型难以适应不同规模和结构的交通网络，缺乏灵活的通用接口。

Method: 设计了一个共享图神经网络，为每个交通运动（movement）分配分数；每个 junction 通过确定性关联矩阵将分数转换为可变大小的合法信号相位集合；使用有向走廊节点提供交通上下文，运动节点表示受控的输入到输出路径；采用类型化均值聚合（typed mean aggregation）为每个运动生成一个标量；相位定义和信号时序保持在学习的网络之外，从而解耦图大小和 junction 动作数量与参数形状。

Result: PPO实验表明：1）在未见过的合成网格几何上策略性能得以保持；2）信号覆盖变化暴露了对分布偏移的敏感性；3）单个城市策略在五个异构城市图上执行产生异构结果。

Conclusion: 该接口实现了交通信号控制中图结构与学习参数的解耦，提供了跨网络迁移的可行性证据，但尚不能泛化为任意路网迁移的一般性估计。

Abstract: We present a traffic-signal control interface in which a shared graph neural network assigns scores to individual traffic movements. Each junction converts these scores into its own variable-sized set of legal signal phases using a deterministic incidence matrix. Directed corridor nodes provide traffic context, while movement nodes represent controlled input-to-output paths through junctions. Typed mean aggregation produces one scalar per movement; phase definitions and signal timing remain outside the learned network. This makes graph size and junction-specific action count independent of the learned parameter shapes. PPO experiments evaluate the interface on unseen synthetic grid geometries, altered signal coverage, and five heterogeneous city graphs. The policies retained performance across unseen geometries within the synthetic grid family, while changes in signal coverage exposed sensitivity to a signal-coverage distribution shift. A single trained city-policy instance executed across all five city graphs, with heterogeneous outcomes. These results provide feasibility evidence rather than a general estimate of transfer to arbitrary road networks.

</details>


### [173] [Searching the Space of Feed-Forward Neural-Network Weight-Update Rules with Fixed Depth Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21855)
*Charles Brum,Edward Finkelstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨符号回归能否发现比标准优化器更优的神经网络权重更新规则，在30个基准/网络组合中，有25个案例发现的规则优于调参后的最优优化器，MSE平均降低44.47%。发现的规则虽无统一形式，但多结合了自适应归一化、动量项、非线性变换和有理表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索符号回归能否自动发现超越人工设计优化器的显式神经网络权重更新规则，旨在以轻量级方式发现更优的优化器变体。

Method: 将候选更新规则表示为基于常见优化器衍生操作数（梯度、动量、自适应梯度、矩估计）的固定深度符号表达式，在30个符号回归基准/神经网络组合上进行符号回归搜索。

Result: 在25/30个案例中发现了超越最佳超参数调优的已知优化器的更新规则，改进案例的MSE总和降低44.47%。规则形式多样，但常见模式包括自适应归一化、动量、非线性变换和有理表达式。

Conclusion: 符号回归可作为发现紧凑优化器变体的轻量机制，但需要大规模验证以确认其泛化能力。

Abstract: We investigate whether symbolic regression can discover explicit neural network weight-update rules that outperform standard hand-designed optimizers on small symbolic regression benchmarks. Candidate update rules are represented as fixed-depth symbolic expressions over operands derived from common optimizers, including gradient, momentum, adaptive-gradient, and moment-estimate quantities. Across 30 benchmark/neural network combinations, the symbolic regression procedure found an update rule outperforming the best hyperparameter-tuned established optimizer in 25 cases, with an aggregate MSE reduction of 44.47\% over the improved cases. The discovered rules do not all share a single common symbolic form, but many combine adaptive normalization, momentum-like quantities, nonlinear transformations, and rational expressions. These results suggest that symbolic regression can serve as a lightweight mechanism for discovering compact optimizer variants, while also highlighting the need for larger-scale validation.

</details>


### [174] [LeAct: Learning to Reason from Expert Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21856)
*Ziran Yang,Chengshuai Shi,Raj Ghugare,Benjamin Eysenbach,Karthik Narasimhan,Chi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出LeAct框架，通过将专家系统的动作视为潜在变量，恢复其背后的推理链（CoT），从而将静默专家的知识蒸馏到学生模型中。在不完美信息博弈和机器人模拟基准上，LeAct显著优于直接模仿和专家迭代基线，证明了专家系统可作为基础模型推理教师的新来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推理模型依赖人类标注或强LLM蒸馏获得的推理数据，但专家系统（如游戏引擎、经典规划器、定理证明器）能产生接近最优的动作，却无法提供动作背后的推理链（思维链）。恢复这些推理链可以将静默专家的知识蒸馏到泛化能力更强的学生模型中。

Method: 将专家动作背后的思维链视为潜在变量，提出LeAct（Learning to reason from Actions）框架。学生模型为每个专家动作采样候选CoT，保留那些能显著提高学生模型自身恢复该动作概率的CoT，从而优化这一潜在变量。

Result: 在小型可枚举的不完美信息博弈中达到求解器的数值下限；在大规模博弈中比最强专家迭代基线更接近求解器5倍；在Flop Hold'em（约10^9信息集）上正面交锋获胜+60 mbb/g；在机器人模拟基准上是唯一优于直接模仿的训练方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一个原则性的框架，证明专家系统可以作为基础模型的全新推理教师来源，为从静默专家中蒸馏推理能力提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Modern reasoning models depend on reasoning data, today sourced from human annotations or distilled from stronger LLMs. However, a rich and largely untapped source of supervision lies in expert systems (e.g., game engines, classical planners, theorem provers), which routinely produce near-optimal actions across diverse domains. But these experts are silent: they commit to an action without writing down the chain of thought (CoT) behind it. Recovering that CoT as natural-language reasoning would distill expert knowledge into a student that generalizes beyond the demonstrated actions. We treat it as a latent variable and study how to recover it from the action alone. Our approach, LeAct (Learning to reason from Actions), optimizes this latent variable: the student samples candidate CoTs for each expert action, and we retain those that measurably improve its own probability of recovering the action. Across imperfect-information games at multiple scales and a simulated robotics benchmark, LeAct reaches the solver's numerical floor on small enumerable games. At larger scale, it is $5\times$ closer to the solver than the strongest expert-iteration baseline. At Flop Hold'em ($\sim 10^9$ infosets), LeAct wins head-to-head by $+60$ mbb/g, and on the robotics probe it is the only training recipe that improves on direct imitation. We present a principled framework and the result: expert systems become a categorically new source of reasoning teachers for foundation models.

</details>


### [175] [Variance-Reduced Q-Learning over Static and Time-Varying Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21876)
*Sreejeet Maity,Feng Zhu,Aritra Mitra,Robert W. Heath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VRDQ的基于epoch的分布式Q-learning算法，用于多个智能体在分布式网络中协作学习最优状态-动作值函数。该算法在每个epoch内局部估计Bellman最优算子并通过共识协议扩散信息。在静态和时变网络下，VRDQ均实现了高速率收敛且具有线性加速效果，且仅需$\tilde{O}(1)$的通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体强化学习中，现有分布式Q-learning方法虽然能通过协作提升样本效率，但往往需要较高的通信开销。本文旨在设计一种既能实现线性样本加速、又只需极低通信成本的分布式Q-learning算法。

Method: 提出epoch-based分布式Q-learning算法VRDQ：每个epoch内，智能体局部估计Bellman最优性算子，并通过基于共识的协议进行信息扩散。算法支持静态和时变网络拓扑。

Result: 在静态和时变网络下，VRDQ均具有高概率的有限时间收敛率，实现了线性加速效果；且达到该加速所需的通信成本仅为$\tilde{O}(1)$，显著优于先前工作。

Conclusion: VRDQ证明了在多智能体分布式强化学习中，可以在保持线性样本加速的同时实现极低的通信开销，为通信高效的分布式强化学习提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We investigate a decentralized reinforcement learning problem involving multiple agents that interact with the same Markov Decision Process (MDP). The agents can exchange information over a network to collectively learn the optimal state-action value function. For this setting, we introduce a novel epoch-based distributed $Q$-learning algorithm called VRDQ, where within each epoch, agents locally estimate the Bellman optimality operator and diffuse information using a consensus-based protocol. For both static and time-varying networks, we establish high-probability finite-time convergence rates for VRDQ that enjoy linear speedups from collaboration. Crucially, we prove that such speedups in sample-complexity require only $\tilde{O}(1)$ communication, substantially improving upon the communication costs in prior work.

</details>


### [176] [Remedying Coarsening-Based GNN Training under Heterophily via Adaptive Complementary Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21885)
*Guoming Li,Jian Yang,Xukun Wang,Zixiao Wang,Shangsong Liang,Yifan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE（自适应互补增强）策略，解决现有基于粗化图训练的GNN方法在异嗜性图上性能严重退化的问题。ACE通过学习投影器重建原始节点特征并结合各向异性结构正则化嵌入局部异嗜性，同时采用同方差不确定性加权自适应平衡粗化图训练损失与全图辅助损失。实验表明ACE在异嗜性基准上取得持续提升，在嗜性图上保持竞争力，且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于粗化图训练的GNN方法几乎仅在嗜性图上评估，忽略了更具挑战性的异嗜性设置。研究表明，现有方法在异嗜性图上因粗化过程中不可避免的信息丢失而导致性能显著下降。

Method: ACE是即插即用、模型无关的策略，包含三个关键组件：(1) 学习投影器重建原始节点特征；(2) 各向异性结构正则化嵌入局部异嗜性；(3) 同方差不确定性加权，自适应平衡主粗化图训练损失与全图辅助损失（使用异嗜性感知投影器重建的增强节点特征）。

Result: 大量实验表明ACE在异嗜性基准上取得持续一致的性能提升，同时在嗜性图上保持有竞争力的结果，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: ACE有效解决了粗化训练在异嗜性图上的信息丢失问题，是一种通用且高效的增强策略，为大规模GNN训练在异嗜性场景下的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Coarsening-based training for graph neural networks (GNNs), i.e.\ training on coarsened graphs rather than the original large ones, has become a promising direction for scaling GNNs to massive graphs. However, prior work has been evaluated almost exclusively on \textit{homophilic} graphs, leaving the more challenging \textit{heterophilic} settings underexplored. We show, both empirically and theoretically, that existing coarsening-based training methods suffer significant performance degradation on heterophilic graphs due to inevitable loss of graph information during coarsening. To address this, we propose {\bf A}daptive {\bf C}omplementary {\bf E}nhancement, a plug-and-play, model-agnostic strategy that reintegrates the information discarded in coarsening: ACE learns a projector for re-constructing original node features and applies \textit{anisotropic structural regularization} to embed local heterophily. We further adopt \textit{homoscedastic uncertainty weighting} to adaptively balance the combined training objective of primary coarsened-graph training loss and full-graph auxiliary loss with augmented node features re-constructed by the heterophily-aware projector. Extensive experiments show that ACE drives consistent gains on heterophilic benchmarks while preserving competitive results on homophilic graphs with minimal computational overhead. Code is available at the GitHub repository: https://github.com/vasile-paskardlgm/ACE.

</details>


### [177] [MissHyper: Restoring Clinical Synchronicity in Missingness-Guided Hypergraph Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21922)
*Mingyi Ma,Qingxiong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对临床不规则多变量时间序列中协时间戳结构被过早扁平化的问题，提出MissHyper模型，通过缺失引导的超图预测框架，在消息传递前恢复协时间戳上下文，在多个临床数据集上实现一致的多步预测增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件中心模型在处理同一时间戳获取的多项测量时，过早将其嵌入为孤立节点，导致局部患者状态上下文在后续消息传递层之前不可用，存在预传播表示瓶颈。

Method: 提出MissHyper模型：1）用局部支持密度线索增强每个事件；2）聚合协时间戳记录恢复患者状态上下文；3）用缺失引导的门控自适应融合节点特定证据与恢复的上下文。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV三个数据集上，MissHyper在多步预测中取得一致增益，优于强超图基线。

Conclusion: 改善事件初始化可以在不重新设计下游传播架构的情况下提升稀疏临床预测性能；快照恢复、自适应融合和支持密度编码均有贡献，事件初始化是稀疏临床预测的关键设计轴。

Abstract: Clinical irregular multivariate time series are shaped not only by physiological dynamics but also by the measurement process that determines when and what to observe. In event-centric models, however, co-timestamp structure can be flattened too early: measurements acquired at the same timestamp are embedded as isolated nodes, leaving local patient-state context unavailable until later message-passing layers. We study this pre-propagation representation bottleneck and address it by restoring co-timestamp context before message passing begins. We propose MissHyper, a missingness-guided hypergraph forecasting model with pre-propagation synchronicity restoration. MissHyper augments each event with a local support-density cue, aggregates co-timestamp records to recover patient-state context, and uses a missingness-guided gate to adaptively fuse node-specific evidence with the recovered context. Across PhysioNet 2012, MIMIC-III, and MIMIC-IV, MissHyper achieves consistent gains in multi-step forecasting and outperforms a strong hypergraph baseline. These results suggest that improving event initialization can benefit sparse clinical forecasting without requiring a redesigned downstream propagation architecture. Ablations indicate that snapshot restoration, adaptive fusion, and support-density encoding all contribute, pointing to event initialization as a critical design axis for sparse clinical forecasting.

</details>


### [178] [RIS-Kernel: A Model-Agnostic Architecture for Long-Context LLM Inference via Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21927)
*Anderson R. Santos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出减少交互采样（RIS）推理引擎，通过稀疏随机几何将自注意力复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N log N)，在普通CPU硬件上实现长上下文LLM推理。在Qwen2-1.5B-Instruct上验证：32K tokens时1%密度+70次集成达到75%准确率（超越密集基线71.88%），65K tokens时突破密集注意力的内存限制，检索增益达14.06个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的全自注意力机制计算复杂度为O(N²)，将长上下文文档分析限制在65,536 token以内，且需要昂贵的GPU集群。现有方法无法在普通硬件上高效处理长序列。

Method: 提出模型无关的RIS推理引擎，不修改权重，利用稀疏随机几何将自注意力复杂度降至O(N log N)，在商品内存限制内运行。包括随机采样（Stochastic）和结构化采样（Structural）两种变体，通过多种子集成实现高精度。

Result: 32K tokens：RIS-Stochastic在1%密度+70种子时达75%准确率（超密集基线71.88%），5%密度+10种子持平（71.88%）。65K tokens：RIS突破密集注意力OOM限制，检索增益达14.06个百分点（相对零上下文基线51.56%），McNemar检验p=0.078边缘显著。所有实验在16-128GB RAM的普通无加速CPU服务器上运行。

Conclusion: 稀疏注意力可作为正则化器：低密度多种子过滤序列级噪声，高密度重新引入干扰噪声。RIS证明了在标准学术硬件上无需GPU即可实现长上下文LLM推理的可行性。
